We then conduct a multivocal literature review of 29 sources to identify best practices for authoring SKILL.md files and introduce skill smells as violations of these practices. Finally, we develop an automated detector and apply it to real-world skills, finding that over 99% of SKILL.md files contain at least one skill smell, and once introduced, skill smells rarely disappear as skills evolve. These findings reveal a substantial gap between recommended and actual authoring practices, motivating the development of automated techniques to remediate skill smells while increasing developer awareness of this emerging quality issue.

</details>


### [72] [Kani: A Model Checker for Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01504)
*Rémi Delmas,Zyad Hassan,Qinheping Hu,Rahul Kumar,Felipe R. Monteiro,Thanh Nguyen,Adrián Palacios,Celina Val,Michael Tautschnig,Justus Adam,Daniel Schwartz-Narbonne,Carolyn Zech*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Kani是一个开源的Rust模型检查器，通过将Rust的MIR编译到CBMC引擎，在无需用户标注的情况下自动验证安全属性。它通过契约语言扩展了有界模型检测到无界验证，在工业级Rust项目中发现了6个未知bug，并在Rust标准库验证中达到每次代码变更超过16,000个测试用例的规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rust的所有权类型系统能防止安全代码中的内存错误，但无法保证不安全操作（如裸指针解引用）的正确性、函数正确性以及运行时恐慌的消除。需要一种方法将这些正交的属性纳入编译时验证。

Method: Kani将Rust的MIR编译到CBMC的位精确验证引擎，自动检查安全属性。通过函数契约、循环契约、量词和函数桩组成的规范语言，将有界模型检测扩展到无界验证。

Result: 在工业级Rust项目中成功将验证从防恐慌升级到功能正确性，发现6个先前未知的bug。在Rust标准库验证中达到每次代码变更超过16,000个测试用例的规模。

Conclusion: Kani证明了将带契约的模型检测应用于Rust的可行性，能够在生产CI环境中大规模运行，为Rust代码提供超越传统bug查找的正确性保证。

Abstract: Rust's ownership type system prevents memory errors in safe code, but certain desirable properties remain orthogonal to compilation: the soundness of unsafe operations (e.g., raw pointer dereferences), functional correctness, and absence of runtime panics. We present Kani, an open-source model checker for Rust that pushes bounded model checking beyond bug-finding to provide correctness guarantees for these properties. Kani compiles proof harnesses from Rust's Mid-level Intermediate Representation (MIR) into CBMC's bit-precise verification engine, automatically checking a comprehensive set of safety properties with no user annotation. To extend verification from bounded to unbounded, Kani provides a specification language comprising function contracts, loop contracts, quantifiers, and function stubbing. We demonstrate feasibility through case studies on industrial Rust projects, where contracts upgraded verification from panic-freedom to functional correctness, uncovering six previously unknown bugs. Kani operates at scale in production CI, with over 16,000 harnesses verified per code change in the Rust standard library verification campaign.

</details>


### [73] [A Capacity-Aware Parr Model for Agile Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01562)
*Pedro E. Colla*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Parr模型进行紧凑重构，提出了一种容量感知的敏捷项目预测层。该模型将归一化的Parr形状潜在努力需求与观察或计划的能力轨迹相结合，可预测总体进度、完成时间、能力赤字和能力冗余，无需假设资源限制下遵循相同的内部活动路径。提供了离散冲刺公式、从Scrum记录校准的方法以及滚动起点验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的软件工作量分布模型（如PNR族和Parr曲线）基于隐含的人员配置模式设计，在团队容量固定、部分固定或外部受限的敏捷环境中直接使用受到限制，因为原始曲线规定的人员需求可能超出组织实际可分配的资源。

Method: 对Parr模型进行紧凑重构，将归一化的Parr形状潜在努力需求与观察或计划的能力轨迹相结合，使用少量参数集（总工作量K、Parr形状参数、可匹配非零初始人员的原点常数c、能力轨迹），提供离散冲刺公式、从Scrum记录校准的方法以及滚动起点验证协议。

Result: 该模型可预测总体进度、完成时间、能力赤字和能力冗余，且不假设资源限制下遵循相同的内部活动路径。提供了与简单管理基线的验证协议。

Conclusion: 提出了一种比完整因果理论更窄、更实用的容量感知预测层，解决了经典模型在敏捷环境下因容量限制而不可用的问题。

Abstract: Classical software effort distribution models, including the PNR family and Parr alter native curve, were designed to describe the time distribution of development effort under an implied staffing pattern. Their direct use in agile environments is limited when team capacity is fixed, partially fixed, or externally constrained, the original curve may prescribe a staff demand that the organization cannot allocate. This paper proposes a compact refactoring of Parr model as a capacity-aware forecasting layer for agile projects. The contribution is deliberately narrower than a full causal theory of project dynamics. A normalized Parr shaped latent effort demand is combined with an observed or planned capacity trajectory. The resulting model forecasts aggregate progress, completion time, capacity deficit, and capacity slack without assuming that the same internal activity path is followed under resource restriction. The model uses a small parameter set such as total effort K, a Parr shape parameter, an origin constant c that can match nonzero initial staffing, and the capacity trajectory. A discrete sprint formulation is provided, together with a calibration method from ordinary Scrum records and a rolling origin validation protocol against simple management baselines.

</details>


### [74] [A Single Patch Is Not Enough: Deterministic Fusion of Repair Candidates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01597)
*Boyang Yang,Xiangliang Hu,Luyao Ren,Yanjun Chen,Bach Le,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé,Haoye Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PatchFusion提出了一种确定性的原子证据融合方法，不依赖测试结果，通过复用编辑原子证据来选择和构造最终补丁，在多个基准测试上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码智能体的评估使用pass@k，但实际开发中只应用单个补丁，存在pass@k到pass@1的差距。现有后处理方法仅排序、过滤或用LLM评判，没有确定性复用共享编辑原子证据来同时选择和构造最终补丁。

Method: PatchFusion首先将全补丁的一致性融合进修复邻域，选择可审计的代表性补丁，然后应用证据约束融合（ECF）保留重复的编辑原子并剪枝不支持的部分。同时构建了PatchFuseBench固定池基准。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified解决426/500个bug，SWE-bench Multilingual解决236/300个，Defects4J达到87/371个合理补丁，在所有三个基准上优于所有匹配的候选池选择器。ECF增加了5/6/9个已解决bug且无回归，达到候选可达上限的96.2%和89.7%。

Conclusion: 跨候选证据能以远低于测试和LLM基序的成本恢复更多正确补丁，且增益在候选池重采样时保持稳定。

Abstract: Modern LLM coding agents are commonly evaluated using pass@k, but developers typically apply a single final patch in real-world settings. This pass@k-to-pass@1 gap is a post-generation problem: a candidate patch pool may contain a correct patch, but the system must decide which one to suggest to developers. Existing post-generation approaches mainly rank whole candidates, filter them with tests, or query an LLM judge, but none deterministically reuse shared edit-atom evidence to both select and construct the final patch. Thus, we propose PatchFusion, a deterministic atomic evidence fusion approach for candidate patches that consults no test outcome at decision time. PatchFusion first fuses whole-diff agreement into a repair neighborhood, selects an auditable representative, and then applies evidence-constrained fusion (ECF) to retain repeated edit atoms and prune unsupported parts. To evaluate this setting, we build PatchFuseBench, a fixed-pool benchmark covering SWE-bench Verified, SWE-bench Multilingual, and Defects4J candidate patches.
  On PatchFuseBench, PatchFusion solves 426/500 bugs on SWE-bench Verified and 236/300 on SWE-bench Multilingual, and reaches 87/371 plausible patches on Defects4J, outperforming every matched candidate-pool selector on all three. PatchFusion recovers 41 and 27 bugs that no single source solves (30 and 18 more over the best single source). Ablation studies show that ECF adds +5/+6/+9 solved bugs by recovering in-pool repairs that selection misses, with no observed regression, and that PatchFusion's gains remain stable as candidate pools are resampled. On these complementary multi-source pools, cross-candidate evidence recovers more correct patches than the test-based and LLM-based selectors we evaluate, at orders-of-magnitude lower cost, reaching within 96.2% and 89.7% of the candidate-reachable ceiling on the two SWE-bench benchmarks.

</details>


### [75] [AgentFlow: Building Agent Dependency Graphs for Static Analysis of Agent Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01640)
*Shenao Wang,Xinyi Hou,Yanjie Zhao,Xiao Cheng,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentFlow——首个用于从智能体程序中恢复和分析智能体依赖关系的静态分析框架。它构建了智能体依赖图(ADG)，一种框架无关的图表示，可捕获智能体、提示、模型、能力、记忆状态和控制策略等实体及其依赖关系。基于ADG，AgentFlow支持智能体物料清单(BOM)生成和提示到工具风险检测。在5个框架、5,399个真实世界智能体程序上的评估表明，AgentFlow能恢复比现有AST工具更丰富的实体和依赖，发现238个提示到工具的污染风格风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体程序不仅依赖传统的控制流和数据流，还依赖一类新型的"智能体依赖关系"，这些依赖通常通过框架语义（如智能体构造器、工具装饰器、智能体交接声明）表达，现有静态分析或依赖追踪工具难以恢复这些依赖。

Method: 提出AgentFlow静态分析框架，构建框架无关的Agent Dependency Graph (ADG)，将智能体、提示、模型、能力、记忆状态、控制策略表示为类型化节点，将组件依赖、控制流、数据流依赖表示为类型化边。基于ADG支持Agent BOM生成和提示到工具风险检测等分析。在五个代表性智能体框架上实现，并在包含5,399个真实程序的AgentZoo语料库上评估。

Result: 相比现有AST智能体静态分析工具，AgentFlow能恢复更丰富的智能体实体和依赖；生成更具依赖感知能力的Agent BOM；在真实智能体程序中发现了238个污染风格的提示到工具风险。

Conclusion: ADG为理解、治理和保障新兴智能体软件提供了实用的基础。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly developed as source-code applications built on agent frameworks. These agent programs combine conventional host-language code with framework-defined semantics for models, prompts, tools, memory, and multi-agent orchestration logic. As a result, their behavior depends not only on traditional control and data flows, but also on a new class of agent dependencies. Such dependencies are often expressed as framework-induced semantics, such as agent constructors, tool decorators, and agent handoff declarations, making them difficult to recover with existing static analysis or dependency tracking tools. In this paper, we present AgentFlow, the first static analysis framework for recovering and analyzing agent dependencies from agent programs. AgentFlow constructs an Agent Dependency Graph (ADG), a framework-agnostic graph representation that represents agents, prompts, models, capabilities, memory states, and control policies as typed nodes, and captures their component-dependency, control-flow, and data-flow dependencies as typed edges. Built on ADGs, AgentFlow supports a range of analyses for agent governance and security, including Agent Bill of Materials (BOM) generation and prompt-to-tool risk detection. We implement AgentFlow for five representative agent frameworks and evaluate it on AgentZoo, a corpus of 5,399 real-world agent programs. Our evaluation shows that AgentFlow recovers richer agent entities and dependencies than existing AST-based agent static analysis tools, generates more dependency-aware Agent BOMs, and uncovers 238 taint-style prompt-to-tool risks in real-world agent programs. These results show that ADG provides a practical foundation for understanding, governing, and securing emerging agent software.

</details>


### [76] [When Agents Do Not Stop: Uncovering Infinite Agentic Loops in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01641)
*Xinyi Hou,Shenao Wang,Yanjie Zhao,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出IAL-Scan，一种用于检测大语言模型智能体项目中无限智能体循环（IALs）故障的静态分析工具。通过对6,549个仓库的分析，IAL-Scan报告了74个潜在发现，经人工确认有68个真实IAL故障，精确率达91.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过迭代执行（规划、工具调用、状态更新、智能体协作）实现自动化，但反馈路径未有效限定时会产生无限智能体循环（IALs），导致成本耗尽、模型拒绝服务、上下文增长及外部副作用等问题，亟需检测手段。

Method: IAL-Scan将异构智能体代码抽象为框架无关的Agent IR，构建智能体循环依赖图（ALDG）恢复显式和框架引入的反馈路径，并检验这些路径能否无有效边界地重复到达高成本或状态增长操作。

Result: 在6,549个LLM智能体仓库上评估，报告74个潜在发现，人工确认68个IAL故障（涉及47个项目），精确率91.9%。

Conclusion: IAL-Scan能有效检测实际LLM智能体项目中的无限循环故障，具有高精确度，为解决智能体系统可靠性问题提供了静态分析方案。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on iterative execution to solve tasks through planning, tool use, state updates, and agent collaboration. While this design enables flexible automation, it also creates a new class of failures: an agent may repeatedly execute model calls, tools, workflow transitions, or agent handoffs when the feedback path is not effectively bounded. We call this problem Infinite Agentic Loops (IALs). IALs are not ordinary programming loops; they arise from the interaction between agent logic, framework semantics, runtime observations, and termination mechanisms. Such failures can amplify a single request into long running model and tool execution, causing cost exhaustion, model denial of service, context growth, and repeated external side effects. We propose IAL-Scan, a static analysis tool for detecting IAL failures in real-world LLM agent projects. IAL-Scan abstracts heterogeneous agent code into a framework independent Agent IR, builds an Agentic Loop Dependence Graph (ALDG) to recover explicit and framework induced feedback paths, and checks whether these paths can repeatedly reach costly or state growing operations without an effective bound. We evaluate IAL-Scan on 6,549 LLM agent repositories. It reports 74 potential findings, among which manual review confirms 68 IAL failures across 47 projects, achieving 91.9% precision.

</details>


### [77] [Refploit: Facilitating Exploit Construction via Code-Agent Trajectory Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01760)
*Zirui Chen,Zhipeng Xue,Jiayuan Zhou,Xing Hu,Xin Xia,Xiaohu Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Refploit是一个基于LLM的轨迹恢复框架，通过差分执行验证和约束引导恢复，从公开的漏洞利用参考中自动复现可运行的Java漏洞利用代码。在143个漏洞的172个参考上达到80.2%的复现率，比初始轨迹相对提升64.3%，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体在自动复现Java库漏洞利用时，生成的代码往往看似成功但实际未触发漏洞逻辑（如用自实现函数替换脆弱API）。自动将不完整、非结构化的漏洞利用参考转化为可运行利用代码仍面临挑战。

Method: Refploit框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 差分执行验证——判断智能体生成的利用是否真正触发了脆弱逻辑；(2) 复现进度分析——定位轨迹中已完成子任务和误导方向；(3) 约束引导恢复——基于分析结果推导约束条件，引导LLM对失败轨迹进行聚焦恢复。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上，Refploit成功复现138个漏洞利用（复现率80.2%），相对初始轨迹提升64.3%；优于PoCGen和GPT-5.4驱动的Codex等基线方法；在不同代码智能体上均表现出一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 失败的智能体轨迹并非完全无用，其中包含已完成子任务和应避免的误导方向。通过差分执行验证和约束引导恢复，可以有效提升漏洞利用复现的成功率和可靠性，且该方法具有较好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Vulnerability exploits play a crucial role in assessing the downstream impact of Java library vulnerabilities. While some vulnerabilities are accompanied by disclosed exploit references, automatically reproducing such references into runnable exploits remains challenging because they are often incomplete, unstructured, or only describe partial reproduction steps. Recent code agents provide a promising way to automate this process, but our study shows that their generated exploits often appear successful without triggering the actual vulnerable logic, such as replacing vulnerable APIs with self-implemented functions. To address this, we propose Refploit, an LLM-based trajectory recovery framework for facilitating vulnerability reproduction from public exploit references. The key insight is that a failed agent trajectory is not entirely useless. It may have already completed some reproduction subtasks while also revealing misleading directions that should be avoided. Refploit first validates an agent-generated exploit through differential execution. When the exploit is ineffective, Refploit analyzes its reproduction progress, locates the trajectory segments associated with the reproduction progress, and derives constraints to guide focused recovery. We evaluate Refploit on three open-source Java vulnerability datasets, covering 172 exploit references for 143 vulnerabilities. Under DeepSeek-V4-Flash, Refploit successfully reproduces 138 exploits, achieving a reproduction rate of 80.2%. It achieves a 64.3% relative improvement over the initially generated trajectories and outperforms both the SOTA exploit-generation method PoCGen and advanced code agents such as Codex with GPT-5.4. We further adapt Refploit to another code agent and observe consistent improvements, demonstrating its generality.

</details>


### [78] [KRCA: An Efficient Root Cause Analysis System in Hyper-Scale Microservice Systems via Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01788)
*Jiamin Jiang,Jingfei Feng,Yu Luo,Qingliang Zhang,Yongqian Su,Wenwei Gu,Shenglin Zhang,Tianyu Cui,Yao Wu,Jielong Huang,Nan Qi,Dan Pei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: KRCA是一个面向超大规模微服务系统的端到端根因分析系统，采用多阶段流水线（API级下钻→骨架因果图→记忆增强多智能体框架）实现根因定位与故障分类，在快手生产环境部署半年以上，诊断准确率AC@1达0.88和0.79，平均诊断时间降低77.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大规模微服务系统存在极端的动态性和巨大的规模，现有根因分析方法难以适应这种复杂环境，亟需高效、准确的自动化RCA系统来帮助SRE快速定位根因服务并分类故障类型。

Method: KRCA采用多阶段流水线：（1）API级下钻以隔离可疑服务，管理巨大的搜索空间；（2）从异常指标实例化基于骨架的因果图，作为高召回的结构先验；（3）利用记忆增强的多智能体框架验证因果关系并生成最终故障报告，将结构化因果约束与多智能体推理相结合。

Result: 根因服务定位AC@1达0.88，故障类型分类AC@1达0.79，相比最强基线绝对提升至少31%。在快手生产环境部署超6个月，平均诊断时间降低77.3%。

Conclusion: KRCA通过结合结构化因果约束与多智能体推理，在诊断准确性与实时生产环境的效率需求之间取得了良好平衡，是超大规模微服务系统中有效的端到端RCA解决方案。

Abstract: Hyper-scale microservice systems have become the standard infrastructure for large-scale Internet companies. These systems consist of numerous loosely coupled microservices that evolve independently through continuous development and deployment. Such complexity makes failures unavoidable, necessitating efficient Root Cause Analysis (RCA) to help Site Reliability Engineers (SREs) quickly localize root cause services and classify failure types. However, existing RCA methods often struggle to adapt to the extreme dynamism and massive scale of these systems. In this paper, we present KRCA, an end-to-end RCA system designed for hyper-scale microservice systems. To manage the vast search space, KRCA employs a multi-stage pipeline that begins with an API-level drilldown to isolate suspicious services. It then instantiates a skeleton-based causal graph from anomalous metrics to serve as a high-recall structural prior, before utilizing a memory-augmented multi-agent framework to verify causality and generate the final failure report. By combining structured causal constraints with multi-agent reasoning, KRCA employs balances diagnostic accuracy with the efficiency requirements of real-time production use. Experimental results show that KRCA achieves AC@1 scores of 0.88 and 0.79 for root cause service localization and failure type classification, outperforming the strongest baseline by at lease 31% in absolute gains. KRCA has been deployed in Kuaishou's production environment for over six months, reducing the average diagnosis time by 77.3%.

</details>


### [79] [Archer: Towards Agentic Review for Compiler Optimizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01808)
*Yunbo Ni,Shaohua Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Archer，首个面向编译器优化的自动化代码审查智能体工具。通过在LLVM上评估，发现21%的开放PR和11%的已合入PR存在语义错误（如误编译），揭示了大型编译器项目中关键审查能力的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代编译器频繁更新，但专家审查能力极其有限，导致错误延迟集成甚至引入微妙的语义错误。自动化代码审查虽可能可行，但编译器复杂性带来严峻挑战。

Method: 以LLVM为目标编译器，提出Archer——首个自动化智能体代码审查工具。通过obligations约束分析过程，并用确定性验证守卫仅接收有可执行证据支持的发现。

Result: 在LLVM的70个开放PR和328个已合入PR上评估：Archer发现21%的开放PR和11%的已合入PR存在语义错误（如误编译）。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了大型编译器项目中关键审查能力的严重缺口，展示了Archer作为额外审阅者的实践价值。

Abstract: Modern compilers are frequently updated, but expert review capacity is highly limited, leading to delayed integration and, in some cases, subtle semantic bugs entering the compiler codebase. Automating the code review process with modern general code review agents may be feasible, but it faces critical challenges due to compiler complexity. In this paper, we use LLVM as our target compiler and present Archer, the first automated agentic code review tool for compiler optimizations. Archer constrains the agentic review process from both ends by using obligations to guide analysis and a deterministic validation guard to admit only findings backed by executable evidence.
  We evaluated Archer on 70 open PRs and 328 closed PRs in LLVM from the last two months. The review results are shocking and concerning: Archer discovers that 21% of open PRs and 11% of closed PRs are buggy, i.e, introducing semantic bugs such as miscompilations in LLVM. Our findings expose a critical gap in the capacity for critical review in large compiler projects and demonstrate the practical value of Archer as an additional reviewer.

</details>


### [80] [Decoupling Code Complexity from Newcomer Participation: A Causal Study of AI Coding Agent Adoption in OSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01810)
*Weiwei Xu,Xuanning Cui,Hengzhi Ye,Minghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过双重差分法分析1,888个采用AI编码代理的开源项目，发现代理的引入并未挤占新人贡献者的流入、入职和留存，尽管代码复杂度有所上升（Python认知复杂度+11%，圈复杂度+3-4%），但新人参与并未因此减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源项目依赖新人持续流入，但AI编码代理（如Cursor、Claude Code）可能通过吸收简单任务和使代码更难阅读来挤占新人机会。本文旨在因果性地回答这一担忧是否成立。

Method: 使用GitHub代码搜索识别出1,888个首次提交代理配置文件的采用项目，通过倾向得分匹配找到非采用控制组，采用双重差分法（DID）进行因果推断，主要分析聚焦于603个有真实采用前期的项目。测量指标包括新人流入、留存、入职率以及代码复杂度（认知复杂度和圈复杂度）。

Result: 未发现挤占效应：新人流入无显著下降（点估计从小幅增加到最保守趋势下的轻微不显著下降），入职和留存不变，good-first-issue标签无下降。代码复杂度确实上升（Python认知复杂度+11%，约为此前估计的四分之一；各类语言圈复杂度+3-4%），但在复杂度上升的子集中，新人参与并未下降。

Conclusion: 在成熟开源项目中，采用AI编码代理虽使代码复杂度适度增加，但并未挤占项目所依赖的人类新人贡献者——AI辅助与人类参与之间的担忧性权衡并未实际出现。

Abstract: Open-source projects depend on a steady inflow of newcomers. A growing concern is that AI coding agents (tools such as Cursor and Claude Code that write code from natural-language instructions) will crowd them out, by absorbing the simple tasks that beginners start with and by making code harder to read. We give this concern a causal answer. Using GitHub code search we identify 1,888 projects that adopted an agent, signaled by their first commit of a configuration file. We apply difference-in-differences against matched non-adopting controls, restricting the main analysis to the 603 adopters with a genuine pre-adoption period. We find no evidence of crowding-out: across estimators newcomer inflow shows no significant decline after adoption (point estimates run from a small increase to, under the most conservative trend specification, a slight and insignificant dip), onboarding and retention are unchanged, and a sparse, correlational beginner-task measure (good-first-issue labels, which we cannot test for parallel trends) shows no decline. The feared mechanism is real but decoupled: adoption raises per-function code complexity (about +11% on a cognitive metric for Python, a quarter of the prior estimate, and +3 to 4% in cyclomatic terms across all languages), yet in fixed-unit subsets where complexity rose (Python on the cognitive metric, and all languages on the cyclomatic metric), newcomer participation does not decline. These results suggest that, in established open-source projects, adopting an AI coding agent makes code modestly more complex but does not crowd out the human newcomers that a project depends on: the feared trade-off between AI assistance and human participation does not materialize.

</details>


### [81] [Understanding Software Defect Prediction: A Large-scale Empirical Study Across Uncertainty Quantification and Performance Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01842)
*Ranjun Peng,Xuan Xie,Rubing Huang,Wenbin He,Zhijie Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究，系统评估了软件缺陷预测中基于概率的不确定性量化（UQ）与性能及校准之间的关系。研究发现UQ高度依赖上下文：在项目内预测中，UQ与假阳性率和AUC的相关性优于与MCC、F1等其他指标；而在跨项目预测中，部分UQ与性能、校准的相关性减弱甚至逆转。结论建议UQ应针对具体性能目标评估，校准需使用多指标独立评估，跨项目转移的概率需重新验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管软件缺陷预测（SDP）分类器输出概率被广泛用于优先级排序、阈值调优和风险沟通，但基于概率的不确定性量化（UQ）指标能否可靠指示模型性能和校准质量仍不清楚，缺乏系统性的大规模实证验证。

Method: 开展了大规模实证研究，评估了5种UQ指标、6种性能指标和3种校准指标，涵盖16种代表性分类器。在两种预测设置下分析：项目内缺陷预测（WPDP，使用36个基准数据集）和跨项目缺陷预测（CPDP，使用32个特征兼容数据集）。

Result: UQ高度依赖上下文。WPDP下，UQ与假阳性率和AUC的相关性比与MCC、F1等更一致，且相关性因分类器类别和数据集不同而异。性能与校准相关但不互换，强判别力分类器可能仍有大校准误差。CPDP下，部分UQ-性能及UQ-校准相关性减弱或逆转，表明不确定性信号不能可靠跨项目迁移。

Conclusion: UQ应针对具体性能目标进行评估；校准应使用多指标独立评估；跨项目转移的概率在使用前需重新验证以确保质量保证决策的可靠性。

Abstract: Software defect prediction (SDP) classifiers produce probabilities used for inspection prioritization, threshold tuning, and risk communication. Probability-based uncertainty quantification (UQ) characterizes prediction confidence, but whether common UQ metrics reliably indicate performance and calibration remains unclear. We conducted a large-scale empirical study of probability-based UQ for SDP. We evaluated five UQ metrics, six performance metrics, and three calibration metrics for 16 representative classifiers. We analyzed these relationships under two prediction settings: within-project defect prediction (WPDP), using 36 benchmark datasets, and cross-project defect prediction (CPDP), using 32 feature-compatible datasets. Results showed that UQ was highly context-dependent. Under WPDP, UQ correlated more consistently with false positive rate and AUC than with MCC, F1 score, and other metrics; these correlations also varied across classifier categories and dataset collections. Performance and calibration were related but not interchangeable; classifiers with strong discrimination could still exhibit large calibration error. Under CPDP, several UQ-performance and UQ-calibration correlations weakened or reversed, indicating that uncertainty signals do not reliably transfer across projects. Thus, UQ should be evaluated against specific performance objectives. Calibration should be assessed independently using multiple metrics. Transferred probabilities should be revalidated before guiding quality-assurance decisions.

</details>


### [82] [Technical Debt Friction for Maintenance Prioritization: An Industrial Multi-Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01850)
*Simeon Tverdal,Phu Nguyen,Arda Goknil,Antonio Martini,Markus Borg,Adam Tornhill*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出技术债务摩擦（technical debt friction）作为识别软件维护痛点的新概念，通过多案例工业研究验证其有效性，发现该概念有助于定位维护负担，但需结合上下文和技术视角使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件分析（如代码健康度、热点、耦合度）虽能提供价值信号，但无法充分捕捉实际软件演化中因变更带来的负担（experienced burden of change），需要更有效的优先排序概念来指导维护和重构投入。

Method: 在多个工业案例中开展结构化访谈（structured walkthrough sessions），使用代码健康度、热点、耦合度、重构目标和社会技术视图等分析产物，与从业者共同评估技术债务摩擦概念的实用性。

Result: 从业者普遍认为技术债务摩擦有助于推理维护负担；文件层面摩擦常与已知问题区域及后续维护文件一致；项目层面摩擦分布可揭示更广的维护与演化模式；但实际价值高度依赖上下文。

Conclusion: 技术债务摩擦作为决策支持概念具有前景，但最有效的是结合上下文知识与补充证据使用。

Abstract: Software-intensive organizations need effective ways to identify where maintenance and refactoring efforts will yield the greatest practical benefit. Although software analytics such as code health, hotspots, and coupling provide valuable signals, they do not always capture the experienced burden of change that slows software evolution in practice. This paper presents a multi-case industrial study of technical debt friction as a prioritization-oriented concept for identifying where technical debt most strongly affects maintenance and evolution. We investigate how practitioners interpret the concept, whether friction-related analysis aligns with perceived maintenance pain points and refactoring needs, and what broader maintenance and evolution insights friction can provide beyond individual refactoring candidates. To this end, we conducted structured walkthrough sessions with practitioners across multiple industrial cases using analysis artifacts including code health, hotspots, coupling, refactoring targets, and socio-technical views. Our findings show that practitioners generally considered technical debt friction useful for reasoning about maintenance burden, especially when interpreted together with complementary technical and socio-technical views. At the file level, friction often aligned with known problematic areas and, in several cases, with files that later received maintenance attention, although its practical relevance depended strongly on context. In addition, our exploratory project-level analysis suggests that friction distributions may reveal broader maintenance and evolution patterns. These results indicate that technical debt friction is promising as a decision-support concept, but most effective when used with contextual knowledge and supporting evidence.

</details>


### [83] [Regression Accumulation in Multi-Turn LLM Programming Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01855)
*Yonghui,Huang,Lin Ma,Amjed Tahir,Qian Zhang,Liwen Xiao,Lysa Xiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在多轮LLM编程对话中，40%-73%的任务会出现回归累积现象（后轮代码破坏前轮需求）。主要故障类是跨轮冲突。唯一有效的缓解策略是验证门机制（检查新代码是否通过先前测试），可将DeepSeek-V3的最终轮质量从75.8%提升至87.9%，Llama-3.1-8B从31.6%提升至47.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM辅助编程评估多聚焦于单轮对话，但实际开发中编程是多轮迭代的。后轮代码可能破坏前轮已满足的需求（回归累积），导致可靠性高估。需要系统研究多轮编程中的回归问题。

Method: 从HumanEval+和MBPP+构建542个任务，每个任务扩展为8轮需求演化链。评估6个LLM共26,016个实例。每轮测试当前代码是否仍通过先前基准测试。对384个失败案例进行独立四标注者分类，构建多轮回归缺陷分类体系。

Result: 所有6个模型均出现回归累积现象，40%-73%的任务在完整对话中丢失了之前正确行为。最终轮质量低于初始轮。跨轮冲突是主要失败类。验证门机制是唯一能一致提升所有模型的策略，使DeepSeek-V3最终轮质量从75.8%提升至87.9%，Llama-3.1-8B从31.6%提升至47.3%。

Conclusion: 强单轮性能可能高估多轮编程对话的可靠性。未来评估和工具设计应测试后轮代码建议是否保留前轮需求，并应包含验证门机制。

Abstract: In LLM-assisted software development, coding is often iterative. We study regression accumulation in multi-turn LLM programming conversations, where later code suggestions may break requirements introduced in earlier turns. Reliability therefore depends not only on satisfying the current request, but also on preserving previously satisfied behavior.
  We construct 542 tasks from HumanEval+ and MBPP+ and extend each task into an 8-turn requirement-evolution chain. We evaluate six LLMs on 26,016 turn instances (542 x 6 x 8). At each turn, we test whether the current code still passes earlier benchmark tests. We also analyze 384 failure cases from the failure population and build a taxonomy of multi-turn regression bugs through independent four-annotator labeling.
  Our results show that regression accumulation appears across all six models: 40% to 73% of tasks lose previously correct behavior over the full conversation. Final-turn quality is lower than initial-turn quality across models, especially when later turns add input validation or broader input types. Manual analysis shows that Cross-Turn Conflict, where later code conflicts with earlier requirements, is the main failure class. We further find that Verification Gate, which checks new code against prior tests and triggers rollback and retry, is the only strategy that consistently improves all models, raising final-turn quality from 75.8% to 87.9% on DeepSeek-V3 and from 31.6% to 47.3% on Llama-3.1-8B.
  These findings suggest that strong single-turn performance can overestimate reliability in multi-turn coding conversations. Future evaluation and tool design should test whether later code suggestions preserve earlier requirements and should include Verification Gate mechanisms.

</details>


### [84] [An Exploratory Study on LLM-Generated Code and Comments in Code Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01867)
*Yongyi Ji,Jiaji Wang,Yi Zhou,Fuxiang Chen,Hongji Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过检测器代理分析方法，对2021-2025年间活跃的公司与社区维护仓库中的LLM生成代码和注释进行了大规模实证研究。结果表明：检测为LLM生成的代码随时间推移减少，且频繁出现在测试用例中；注释比例保持相对稳定；LLM生成代码存在大量仓库内代码克隆，而注释的语法正确率较低；公司维护仓库中LLM生成代码和注释比例更高；仅少量人工标注的bug与LLM生成代码相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在软件开发中广泛应用（如大型科技公司20%-30%的代码由LLM生成），但业界对LLM生成代码和注释的实际使用仍存在质疑，尤其是调试时间增加和生成注释的不自然性问题。本研究旨在系统性地分析LLM生成代码和注释的特征、不同维护类型仓库间的差异，以及与bug的关联程度。

Method: 采用多种检测工具和技术，对2021年至2025年间活跃的公司维护和社区维护仓库中的代码和注释进行检测器代理分析，识别LLM生成的内容，并对比分析其特性、时间趋势、仓库类型差异及与bug的关联。

Result: 1) 检测为LLM生成的代码随时间推移减少，且多见于测试用例中；注释比例相对稳定。2) LLM生成代码存在大量仓库内代码克隆；注释中语法正确的句子比例较低。3) 公司维护仓库中LLM生成代码和注释的比例更高。4) 仅小部分人工标注的bug与LLM生成代码存在关联。

Conclusion: LLM生成代码在现实仓库中的使用呈现出下降趋势，且主要集中在测试代码中；公司维护仓库比社区维护仓库更广泛采用LLM生成内容；LLM生成代码的克隆现象显著但实际引入bug的风险较低，缓解了部分关于LLM代码质量的实际担忧。

Abstract: The use of LLMs in software development has become increasingly widespread on tasks such as code generation and summarization. Reports from large technology companies showed that around 20% to 30% of their code are generated by LLMs. However, there remains skepticism about the practical usage of LLM-generated code and comments, such as concerns on more time for debugging the generated code and the unnaturalness of the generated comments. In this paper, we study the code and comments detected as likely to be generated by LLMs and their characteristics, the differences between company- and community-maintained repositories, and how likely bugs are associated with LLM-generated code. We conduct extensive experiments on active company- and community-maintained repositories from 2021 to 2025 using various tools and techniques that detect code and comments generated by LLMs. Based on our detector-based proxy analysis, the results suggest that code detected as likely to be generated by LLMs decreased over time and appeared frequently in test cases, while that of comments remains relatively stable. Proxy results further suggest that code detected as likely to be generated by LLMs shows substantial intra-repository code clones, whereas comments exhibit a relatively low proportion of grammatically correct sentences. In addition, the company-maintained repositories show a higher percentage of code and comments detected as likely to be generated by LLMs, and only a small percentage of the human-labelled bugs are detected as being likely associated with LLM-generated code.

</details>


### [85] [Understanding Build Reproducibility in the F-Droid Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01890)
*Denise Nanni,Julien Malka,Stefano Zacchiroli,Théo Zimmermann,Gabriele d'Angelo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对F-Droid开源应用生态系统的构建可复现性进行了首次实证研究，发现应用版本更新时比特级可复现率稳步上升；但对已确认可复现的18904个应用版本进行重构建时，成功率为83%，失败主因是依赖缺失（占76%）；成功重建的应用中94%仍保持比特级可复现，表明可重建性随时间衰减严重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: F-Droid在应用发布时验证源代码构建的可复现性，但无法保证未来持续可复现。软件生态演化可能导致可复现性退化，影响软件保存与安全，因此需要系统研究可复现性在时间维度上的变化规律。

Method: 分析F-Droid历史可复现性日志，量化整体比特级可复现率的时间变化趋势；选取2018年9月至2026年2月间F-Droid曾确认比特级可复现的18904个应用版本进行重构建实验，评估可重建性与比特级可复现性的保持情况，并分类统计失败原因。

Result: 整体比特级可复现率随新版本发布稳步上升；重构建成功率为83%，失败案例中76%由依赖缺失导致；成功重建的应用中94%仍保持比特级可复现。

Conclusion: 比特级可复现性在可重建的应用中保持良好，但可重建性本身对时间衰减高度敏感，依赖缺失是主要威胁。

Abstract: The security of open source applications benefits considerably from the possibility of rebuilding their source and verifying the output. F-Droid, a prominent distribution for open source Android applications, systematically rebuilds them from source and tests their bitwise reproducibility at app publishing time. However, F-Droid offers no guarantee that app reproducibility will continue to hold in the future. As software ecosystems evolve, reproducibility may degrade, with potential negative consequences for software preservation and security. We present the first empirical study of build reproducibility in the F-Droid app ecosystem. Analyzing historical reproducibility logs, we find that the overall bitwise reproducibility rate has been steadily increasing over time (as new versions of apps are published). We then evaluate how reproducibility holds in time for fixed app versions, by attempting to rebuild 18 904 app versions that F-Droid had previously confirmed bitwise reproducible, published between September 2018 and February 2026, achieving an 83% rebuild success rate, and identify missing dependencies as the dominant cause of failure, accounting for 76% of non-rebuildable cases. Among successfully rebuilt apps, 94% are also bitwise reproducible-i.e., they still yield bitwise identical artifacts upon rebuild. Together, these results show that while bitwise reproducibility largely holds for apps that can be rebuilt, rebuildability itself is highly sensitive to temporal decay.

</details>


### [86] [AI Writes Faster Than Humans Can Review: A Longitudinal Study of an Enterprise 2x Mandate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01904)
*Hao He,Shyam Agarwal,Yegor Denisov-Blanch,Pavel Azaletskiy,Sanmi Koyejo,Bogdan Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对一家中型AI企业2024年1月至2026年4月间802名开发者及196,212个合并请求的面板数据研究，发现强制采用AI编码工具后，人均吞吐量最终翻倍（达到基准线的2.09倍），这一增益主要通过AI工具的采用和累积使用实现，而非强制政策本身。增益普遍分布于各资历开发者，但集中于新代码；同时代码评审结构发生重塑，自动化评审超越人工评审，但合并率和回滚率保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业强制采用AI编码工具的现象日益普遍且报告了巨大的生产力提升，但缺乏纵向证据来阐明这种强制政策如何具体展开其影响机制。

Method: 采用定量案例研究方法，基于802名开发者和196,212个合并请求的面板数据（2024年1月至2026年4月），使用交错双重差分设计（staggered difference-in-differences）来关联开发者内部增益份额与AI采用及累积使用，并分析代码评审结构变化。

Result: 人均吞吐量达到强制前基准线的2.09倍；增益与AI采用和累积使用正相关，强制政策起催化作用而非直接驱动；增益普遍但集中于新代码；自动化评审超过人工评审，评审者负载翻倍，合并率和回滚率保持稳定。

Conclusion: 强制采用AI编码工具能显著提升开发生产力，其核心机制是促进AI工具的采用和深度使用，而非政策本身；该增益具有广泛性但也有限制（集中在新代码），且会显著改变代码评审的工作流程结构。

Abstract: Enterprises increasingly mandate AI coding tools and report large productivity gains, yet longitudinal evidence on how such a mandate unfolds is scarce. In this paper, we present a quantitative case study of a documented enterprise "2x" mandate at a mid-sized, AI-forward company that has been committed to doubling merged pull requests per engineer since mid-2025. In a panel of 802 developers and 196,212 pull requests (January 2024-April 2026), per-capita throughput eventually doubled, reaching 2.09x the pre-mandate baseline in April 2026, among the largest gains reported from a field deployment of AI coding tools to our knowledge. A staggered difference-in-differences design links the within-developer share of this gain to AI adoption and to a further gain that grows with accumulated use, with the mandate acting as a catalyst rather than a direct driver. Because adoption and usage intensity were not randomly assigned, we read this evidence as strongly implicating an adoption-and-use channel rather than as exact causal attribution. The gain is broadly shared across seniority yet concentrated in newer code and not separable across model generations. Adoption also restructured code review around automation: per-reviewer load roughly doubled and automated review overtook human review, while merge and revert rates held steady.

</details>


### [87] [Beyond Textual Repository Exploration: Dual-Modal Structural Reasoning for Agentic Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01929)
*Jiayi Zhang,Kai Huang,Yang Liu,Chunyang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DUALVIEW是一个双模态结构脚手架框架，通过四种互补的图视图（模块耦合图、函数调用图、类层次图、程序依赖图）将视觉推理引入仓库探索，提升智能体程序修复在长周期复杂代码库上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体程序修复方法依赖顺序的、基于文本的代码导航，在处理大规模长周期仓库时，结构化信息（如模块组织、调用关系、依赖链）需要在交互步骤间反复重建，导致探索漂移和局部化不完全。

Method: 提出DUALVIEW框架，通过模块耦合图、函数调用图、类层次图和程序依赖图四种图视图表示仓库结构，并通过可查询接口同时提供视觉和文本响应，使智能体能直接对代码依赖的持久视觉表示进行推理。

Result: 在SWE-bench Pro和Verified上的评估表明，DUALVIEW在不同智能体架构和模型家族上持续提升问题解决性能。消融实验证明收益不仅来自文本结构信息，更来自仓库依赖的视觉外化表示。

Conclusion: 视觉推理能够有效支持长周期仓库探索，双模态结构脚手架是提升智能体程序修复能力的重要方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic program repair have significantly improved issue resolution by enabling iterative repository exploration. However, existing approaches predominantly rely on sequential, text-based code navigation, which fundamentally limits their ability to reason over large-scale long-horizon repositories with complex and long-range dependencies. As issue-resolution agents traverse repositories through fragmented textual observations, structural information such as module organization, call relationships, and dependency chains must be repeatedly reconstructed across interaction steps, often leading to exploration drift and incomplete localization. We present DUALVIEW, a dual-modal structural scaffolding framework that brings visual reasoning into repository exploration for issue-resolution agents. DUALVIEW represents repository structure through four complementary graph views: Module Coupling Graph (MCG), Function Call Graph (FCG), Class Hierarchy Graph (CHG), and Program Dependence Graph (PDG), and exposes them through a queryable interface with visual and textual responses. Rather than reconstructing repository structure from a sequence of textual observations, agents can directly reason over persistent visual representations of code dependencies, enabling more effective exploration and understanding of long-horizon codebases. We evaluate DUALVIEW on SWE-bench Pro and Verified. Results show that DUALVIEW consistently improves issue-resolution performance across different agent architectures and model families. Further ablation studies demonstrate that the gains arise not only from textual structural information but also from visual externalization of repository dependencies, which better supports long-horizon repository exploration.

</details>


### [88] [Underspecification does not imply Incoherence: The Risks of Semantic Collapse in Coding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01953)
*Cedric Richter,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLM在代码生成中常出现"有害语义坍缩"：当任务描述模糊时，模型不会产生多种语义不同的实现，而是稳定生成一种语义错误但看似连贯的代码，导致行为与用户意图偏离。该现象在MBPP、HumanEval、LiveCodeBench中分别影响超过10%、3%和32%的任务，且通过刻意注入不明确性可使发生率提升5倍以上，暴露了依赖不一致性作为模糊性代理指标的盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战学术界普遍假设——提示模糊会导致LLM生成多种语义不同的实现（即不连贯性），揭示实际中模型更倾向于坍缩到单一错误解释，从而误导正确性评估。

Method: 在MBPP、HumanEval、LiveCodeBench基准测试中分析LLM对模糊任务描述的生成行为，并设计实验人为注入模糊性问题，量化"有害语义坍缩"的发生率。

Result: 有害语义坍缩在MBPP、HumanEval、LiveCodeBench中分别影响超过10%、3%和32%的任务；注入模糊性后发生率提升5倍以上。

Conclusion: 依赖不连贯性作为提示模糊性代理指标的消歧与正确性估计技术存在根本性盲点，需要新的方法检测和缓解有害语义坍缩。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become increasingly effective at generating code when task descriptions are clear and precise. Yet, in practice, user-provided task descriptions are often ambiguous, incomplete, or contradictory, leaving critical aspects of the intended program behavior underspecified. In such cases, multiple behaviorally distinct interpretations may satisfy the description equally well, yet semantically differ in ways that matter/affect the user intent. A natural expectation, often assumed by researchers, is that prompt underspecification manifests as incoherence: When asked multiple times, an LLM produces multiple semantically distinct implementations reflecting the ambiguity of the task description. In this paper, we challenge this assumption. We find that LLMs frequently collapse onto a single incorrect interpretation of the task description, consistently generating coherent but behaviorally misaligned code. We term this failure mode detrimental semantic collapse and find that it affects over 10% of tasks in MBPP, 3% in HumanEval, and 32% of LiveCodeBench, all benchmarks assumed to be well-specified. By deliberately injecting underspecification issues in the benchmark prompts, the rate rises to over 5 times, exposing a fundamental blind spot in disambiguation and correctness estimation techniques that rely on incoherence as a proxy for prompt underspecification.

</details>


### [89] [HTTP REST API Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02442)
*Ran Dubin,Amit Dvir*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出HRAL，一种从网络流量中无监督学习API端点结构和行为的异常检测方法，在不依赖预定义规则或文档的情况下，平均召回率82.07%，F1分数87.24%，结合签名规则可达100%检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: API广泛使用带来显著安全风险，而现有方法高度依赖完整的API文档或预定义规则，在文档不完整或缺失的实际场景中效果有限，亟需一种不依赖文档的通用API安全检测方法。

Method: 提出HTTP REST API Learning (HRAL)，一种无监督异常检测方法，直接从网络流量中建模API端点的结构和行为，无需预定义规则或API文档，通过识别行为偏差来标记潜在威胁。

Result: 在不同OpenAPI文档详细程度下评估，HRAL平均召回率82.07%，F1分数87.24%，显著优于现有替代方案；与OWASP ModSecurity CRS等签名规则结合时达到100%检测率。

Conclusion: HRAL在部分文档化的真实API环境中表现出色，可作为现代API安全解决方案的基础层，有效弥补文档缺失场景下的安全检测空白。

Abstract: Application Programming Interfaces (APIs) are essential in software development, enabling web services, mobile apps, and microservices. However, their widespread use introduces significant security risks, highlighting the importance of API security. This paper presents HTTP REST API Learning (HRAL), a novel unsupervised anomaly detection approach that models the structure and behavior of API endpoints directly from network traffic, without relying on predefined rules or documentation. HRAL enables robust detection of malicious activity by understanding how APIs behave and flagging deviations as potential threats. We evaluate HRAL across varying levels of OpenAPI documentation detail and compare it with existing techniques. HRAL achieves strong performance, with an average recall of 82.07% and an F1-score of 87.24%, significantly outperforming alternatives when API documentation is limited. Moreover, our results approach the effectiveness of full API document definitions. When combined with signature-based rules such as the OWASP ModSecurity CRS, our system achieves 100% detection. These results highlight HRAL's effectiveness in real-world, partially documented API environments and its potential as a foundational layer for modern API security solutions.

</details>


### [90] [Epic-Organized vs. Requirement-Aligned Gherkin: An Empirical Evaluation of LLM-Based Acceptance Criteria Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01980)
*Shahbaz Siddeeq,Mateen Abbasi,Jussi Rasku,Zheying Zhang,François Christophe,Tommi Mikkonen,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于史诗（epic）组织的LLM生成Gherkin BDD验收标准的方法（Timeless），相比传统的需求对齐生成方法，在结构有效性、语义覆盖率和专家评估质量上表现更优，尤其在高层次抽象场景下能提升感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gherkin BDD验收标准的自动化编写是需求工程中的瓶颈，当前方法依赖手动编写，效率低下。

Method: 提出Timeless方法——基于史诗组织的LLM管道，与朴素的零样本LLM基线进行对比。在PURE数据集上的107条需求上进行评估，评估指标包括结构度量、基于TF-IDF和稠密嵌入的自动需求覆盖率，以及4位研究人员的盲审专家评估。

Result: JSON约束的管道在所有生成输出上均产生结构有效场景，零样本基线达到99%结构有效；Timeless语义需求覆盖率达94.3%，基线为92.9%；TF-IDF在史诗组织输出上覆盖率较低，表明词汇度量可能遗漏高层次抽象场景的覆盖；专家评估中，史诗组织策略在正确性（4.61 vs 4.14）、可执行性（4.61 vs 4.07）和完整性（4.31 vs 3.50）上均更优。

Conclusion: 史诗组织的Gherkin生成可以提升感知质量，同时保持可比的语义覆盖率，但结论的泛化需要更广泛的复制验证。

Abstract: Automated authoring of Gherkin Behavior-Driven Development (BDD) acceptance criteria remains a manual bottleneck in requirements engineering. This study investigates whether epic-organized LLM-generated Gherkin produces higher quality and coverage than requirement-aligned generation. We compare our Timeless (an epic-organized LLM pipeline) approach against a naive large language model (LLM) baseline on four requirements documents (107 requirements) from the PURE dataset. Evaluation covers structural metrics, automated requirement coverage via TF-IDF and dense embeddings, and blind expert assessment by four researchers. In our evaluation, the JSON-constrained pipeline produced structurally valid scenarios across all generated outputs, while the zero-shot baseline achieved 99% structural validity. Semantic coverage was comparable to the baseline, with Timeless achieving 94.3% semantic Requirement Coverage Rate compared with 92.9% for the baseline. TF-IDF produced lower coverage scores for the epic-organized output, suggesting that lexical metrics may miss coverage when scenarios paraphrase requirements at a higher level of abstraction. Expert raters prefer the epic-organized strategy on Correctness (4.61 vs 4.14), Executability (4.61 vs 4.07), and Completeness (4.31 vs 3.50). Overall, the results suggest that epic-organized generation can improve perceived Gherkin quality while maintaining comparable semantic coverage, although broader replication is needed before generalizing this finding.

</details>


### [91] [Benchmarking Quantum Software Testing with Scalable Quantum Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02029)
*Yuechen Li,Minqi Shao,Xiyuan Li,Jianjun Zhao,Kai-Yuan Cai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Qolumbina是一个用于可控量子软件测试实验的基准测试基础设施，包含40个来自开源仓库的程序，经过系统化选择、重构、规格说明、测试用例和标准化接口处理，并提出了面向QST的特征化标准，支持可扩展性分析和公平比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子软件测试研究严重依赖小型硬编码或电路级基准测试，而可用的量子程序分散在仓库中且缺乏明确的选择标准，限制了公平比较和系统性可重复性。

Method: 从开源仓库中筛选40个程序，经过系统化选择、重构、添加规格说明、测试用例示例、单元测试和标准化接口，将其转化为可直接用于测试的实验对象；并提出面向QST的特征化标准，从功能、输出行为、开发复杂度和量子特定执行复杂度四个维度刻画量子程序。

Result: Qolumbina覆盖了多样化的测试相关属性，支持超越固定大小电路基准的可扩展性分析；通过两个近期QST方法的对照实验，证明了其在执行成本和缺陷检测研究中的可行性，并揭示了影响QST结果解释的后端依赖效应。

Conclusion: Qolumbina为量子软件测试研究提供了系统化、可复现的基准测试基础设施，弥补了现有基准在规模、标准化和可比较性方面的不足。

Abstract: Quantum software testing (QST) checks whether quantum programs behave according to their intended specifications. A key requirement for QST research is a benchmark that supports rigorous empirical evaluation on programs that are testable and better reflect current software development practices. However, existing studies heavily rely on small hard-coded or circuit-level benchmarks, while available quantum programs are scattered across repositories without clear selection criteria, which limits fair comparison and systematic reproducibility. To this end, we present Qolumbina, a benchmark infrastructure for controlled QST experiments on scalable quantum programs. Qolumbina curates 40 programs from open-source repositories, turns them into test-ready subjects through systematic selection, refactoring, specifications, test case examples, unit tests, and standardized interfaces. We also propose QST-oriented criteria to characterize quantum programs along functionality, output behavior, development complexity, and quantum-specific execution complexity. Using these criteria, our empirical study shows that Qolumbina covers diverse testing-relevant properties and supports scalability analysis beyond fixed-size circuit benchmarks. Through controlled experiments with two recent QST approaches, we demonstrate the feasibility of using Qolumbina for execution-cost and fault-detection studies, and highlight backend-dependent effects that can influence QST result interpretation.

</details>


### [92] [Mitigating Package Hallucinations in Large Language Models via Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02052)
*Shuhan Liu,Yukai Zhao,Xing Hu,Kui Liu,Xiaohu Yang,Xin Xia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BOUND是一个轻量级局部模型编辑框架，通过风险感知定位和边界感知目标来缓解LLM中的包幻觉问题，在包推荐任务中降低79.9%的包级幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件工程任务中可能产生包幻觉（生成不存在或无效的包名），这些幻觉可被攻击者利用进行软件供应链攻击，因此缓解包幻觉对提升LLM辅助软件开发的可靠性和安全性至关重要。

Method: 将包幻觉缓解建模为包有效性边界编辑问题，首先通过风险感知定位策略识别与包幻觉相关的模块，然后使用边界感知目标（强化有效包、抑制幻觉包、保持局部行为）对这些模块应用轻量级LoRA适配器进行编辑。

Result: 在包推荐任务中，编辑提示上包级幻觉率降低79.9%，未见提示上降低65.4%；在代码生成任务中降低12.8%，在pip install推荐中降低34.0%。

Conclusion: BOUND有效降低了包幻觉，同时保留了有效包推荐能力，所学习的包有效性边界可泛化到其他包相关任务，提升了LLM包相关输出的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in software engineering tasks, such as code generation, library recommendation, and dependency configuration. However, recent studies show that LLMs may suffer from package hallucination, where they generate non-existent or invalid package names. These hallucinations can be exploited in software supply chain attacks, as attackers may register malicious packages under hallucinated names. Therefore, mitigating package hallucination is important for improving the reliability and security of LLM-assisted software development. In this paper, we introduce BOUND, a lightweight localized model editing framework for mitigating package hallucinations in LLMs. BOUND formulates package hallucination mitigation as a package-validity boundary editing problem, where the boundary refers to the model's ability to distinguish valid packages from hallucinated package names under a given task context. It first locates modules related to package hallucination through a risk-aware localization strategy, and then edits these modules with lightweight LoRA adapters using a boundary-aware objective that reinforces valid packages, suppresses hallucinated packages, and preserves locality behavior. Experimental results show that BOUND effectively reduces package hallucinations while preserving valid package recommendations. In the package recommendation task, BOUND reduces package-level hallucination rate (Package-HR) by 79.9% on edit prompts and by 65.4% on unseen prompts. The learned package-validity boundary further generalizes to other package-related tasks, reducing Package-HR by 12.8% in code generation and by 34.0% in pip install recommendation. These results show that BOUND refines the package-validity boundary of LLMs and improves the reliability of package-related outputs.

</details>


### [93] [Prompt Coverage Adequacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02057)
*Florian Tambon,Michael Konstantinou,Cedric Richter,Charles Chenouard,Mark Harman,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"提示覆盖充分性"的新型测试覆盖准则，用于评估由LLM和智能体从任务描述生成的代码的测试质量。该方法通过利用LLM的注意力机制衡量测试套件对提示中表达需求的满足程度，实验表明它能比传统代码覆盖发现多30%以上的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM和自主智能体正在将软件开发从编写精确程序转向表达意图和目标，提示成为主要开发工件，传统代码覆盖准则在此新范式下不再适用，需要新的测试指导方法。

Method: 提出Prompt Coverage Adequacy准则，作为传统代码覆盖的类比但操作在提示层面，利用LLM的注意力机制度量测试套件对提示中需求的满足程度；通过注意力增强实现该准则的简单实例化。

Result: 在两个数据集和多个LLM上的实验表明，Prompt Coverage与故障检测有效性相关，用于指导测试生成时可比传统代码覆盖多发现30%以上的故障。

Conclusion: Prompt Coverage Adequacy可作为LLM驱动软件开发新范式下测试度量的基础，弥补传统覆盖准则在此场景下的局限性。

Abstract: In recent years, it has become increasingly evident that large language models (LLMs) and autonomous agents raise the level of abstraction in software development by shifting the focus from writing precise procedures to expressing intents and goals. This paradigm shift introduces new challenges, particularly in how testing should be guided when prompts, rather than code, become primary development artifacts. To address this challenge, we propose Prompt Coverage Adequacy, a novel coverage criterion designed to support the testing of code generated from task descriptions. Prompt Coverage Adequacy serves as an analog to traditional code coverage, but operates at the level of prompts used in LLM and agent-based programming. Specifically, it measures how well a given test suite satisfies the requirements expressed in a prompt by leveraging the attention mechanisms of LLMs. We evaluate a simple instantiation of this criterion, based on attention boosting, across two datasets and multiple LLMs. Our results demonstrate that Prompt Coverage is associated with fault-detection effectiveness and can uncover over 30+% more faults than traditional code coverage when used to guide test generation. These findings suggest that Prompt Coverage Adequacy can serve as a foundation for developing testing metrics better suited to the emerging paradigm of LLM-driven software development, addressing the limitations of classical coverage criteria in this new context.

</details>


### [94] [File-Level Copying Is an Implicit Dependency in Open Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02059)
*Runzhi He,Audris Mockus,Wenhao Yang,Minghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法研究了开源生态系统中文件级复制（未管理的代码复用）的规模和后果。从0.1%的提交样本中提取690,500个复制事件，识别出13种轴向复制形式，发现这些形式是开发者意图的不可靠代理。复制代码经常过时（中位数155天），很少记录可恢复的来源（4.3%）或可检查的版本（2.0%）。安全风险集中在依赖供应商复制形式（88%的CVE风险提交），而许可证风险集中在直接源代码复用（66%的预验证候选）。两种风险都能到达打包软件，且对仅基于声明元数据进行操作的依赖扫描器不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文件级复制是一种广泛但不受管理的软件复用形式，它移除了包管理器为声明依赖提供的四个可观察信号（来源、维护、安全、合规性），且没有机制恢复这些信号，导致供应链可见性降低。

Method: 采用混合方法研究，使用World of Code (WoC)对跨整个开源生态系统的复制进行分析。从0.1%的提交样本中提取690,500个复制事件，保留3,912个带有意图标签的复制提交进行标注。通过轴向编码识别出13种复制形式，并分别分析安全风险和许可证风险。

Result: 识别出13种轴向复制形式（包括依赖供应商、硬件/驱动同步、脚手架、UI资产和直接源代码复用）。硬件/驱动复制78%是分支维护工作，依赖供应商复制70%表示上游绕过。复制代码中位数年龄155天，38.5%超过一年，仅4.3%记录来源，2.0%记录版本。安全风险：17,314个CVE风险复制提交，88%在依赖供应商形式中，80% CVSS>=7.0，上游修复采用率仅47%-84%。许可证风险：41,777个预验证候选，66%在源代码复用形式中，39个验证的高星违规。

Conclusion: 文件级复制导致的可见性缺口具有形式特异性：安全风险和许可证风险集中在互补的复制形式中。这两种风险都能到达打包软件，且对仅基于声明元数据进行操作的依赖扫描器不可见，表明需要新的工具和方法来检测跨仓库复制带来的供应链风险。

Abstract: File-level copying is a widespread but ungoverned form of software reuse. Copying files across repositories reduces supply-chain visibility: it removes the four observable signals a package manager provides for a declared dependency (provenance, maintenance, security, and compliance) with no mechanism to restore them. To characterize the scale and consequences of this unmanaged reuse, we present a mixed-method study of copying across the entire open-source ecosystem using World of Code (WoC). From a 0.1% commit sample, we extract 690,500 copy events and retain 3,912 rationale-bearing copy commits for intent labeling. We show that the 13 axial copy forms, spanning vendored dependencies, hardware/driver synchronization, scaffolding, UI assets, and direct source-code reuse, are unreliable proxies for developer intent: among rationale-bearing commits, hardware/driver copies are predominantly fork-maintenance work (78%), while dependency-vendoring copies more often signal upstream bypass (70%) than offline availability. These visibility gaps are form-specific: security and license risk concentrate in complementary copy forms. Copied sources are frequently stale (median 155 days; 38.5% over one year old) and seldom record a recoverable origin (4.3% documented), let alone a checkable version (2.0% versioned); even vendored copies record where they came from only 10% of the time. Security risk concentrates in vendored dependencies: 17,314 CVE-risk copy commits in the full-WoC graph, 88% in the dependency-vendoring form; 80% score CVSS >= 7.0 and upstream-fix adoption is only 47%-84%. License risk concentrates in direct source-code reuse: 41,777 pre-validation candidates, 66% in the source-code form, with 39 verified high-star violations (kappa = 0.752). Both risks reach packaged software and are invisible to dependency scanners operating on declared metadata alone.

</details>


### [95] [Coding Agents Are Guessing: Measuring Action-Boundary Violations in Underspecified DevOps Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02294)
*Zimo Ji,Zekai Zhang,Congying Xu,Zongjie Li,Yudong Gao,Shuai Wang,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: UnderSpecBench是一个评估编码智能体在DevOps任务中因指令不明确导致越界行为的基准测试，包含69个任务族和2208个提示变体。实验显示，55.8-67.8%的运行存在边界违规行为，指令不明确主要导致智能体"猜测"而非失败，目标不明确显著降低动作质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体基准测试过分强调任务完成率，忽视了在指令不明确（含糊）的情况下智能体可能产生危险行为的问题。实际生产环境中，错误的操作可能导致严重后果，因此需要评估智能体在良性但未明确说明的指令下的行为边界。

Method: 构建了UnderSpecBench基准，包含69个基于文档化事件、CVE或工具行为的任务族，覆盖4个DevOps能力域和9个操作控制面。每个任务保持相同的环境和ground-truth安全动作，沿三个轴（意图清晰度、目标确定性、爆炸半径）变化指令，生成2208个提示变体。使用确定性的、基于副作用的oracle评估安全成功、错误目标和越范围结果。

Result: 在OpenCode、Claude Code和Codex的5种智能体×模型配置下，55.8-67.8%的运行存在至少一次边界违规。指令不明确主要导致智能体"猜测"而非失败。目标不明确显著降低动作质量，而爆炸半径线索对动作倾向几乎没有减少作用。

Conclusion: 以完成为中心的评估可能高估了安全自主性，需要从模型层、工具层和系统层设计缓解措施来提升智能体在模糊指令下的安全性。

Abstract: LLM coding agents are increasingly deployed to act autonomously on real production infrastructure. They execute shell commands, modify repositories, and call operational APIs. However, completing a task is not sufficient for safety. A wrong action can cause severe consequences. Existing agent benchmarks largely emphasize task completion, leaving open how agents behave under benign but underspecified instructions.
  We present UnderSpecBench, a benchmark for measuring action-boundary violations in coding agents (i.e., Claude Code, Codex, and OpenCode) on DevOps tasks. UnderSpecBench includes 69 task families grounded in documented incidents, CVEs, or tool behavior and organized across four DevOps capability domains and nine operational control surfaces. To isolate underspecification from task difficulty, each task keeps the same environment and ground-truth safe action while varying the instruction along three axes: intent clarity, target certainty, and blast radius. The resulting 2,208 prompt variants are evaluated with deterministic, side-effect-based oracles that separate Safe Success, Wrong Target, and OverScope outcomes; non-action runs are further classified as clarification, refusal, or deferment.
  Across five agent x model configurations using OpenCode, Claude Code, and Codex, the evaluation results show that underspecification does not mainly make agents fail; it makes them guess. 55.8-67.8% of runs violate at least one boundary. Target underspecification sharply degrades action quality, while blast-radius cues barely reduce action propensity. These findings show that completion-centric evaluation can overstate safe autonomy and motivate mitigations at the model, harness, and system layer.

</details>


### [96] [Guiding Human Validation of LLM-Generated Code via Verifiable Literate Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02333)
*Ziqi Yuan,Wenhao Lu,Hao Wu,Dunhong Jin,Chuan Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VLP是一个人类参与的框架，通过无歧义的自然语言文档作为中间层，帮助用户验证和修复LLM生成代码中的意图-代码不匹配问题，显著提升代码通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成代码的验证方法存在不足：自动化测试受限于提示歧义和模型易错性；仅让用户参与部分构件（如提示、测试用例）会遗漏边界情况和程序细节。通过bug研究，发现失败常源于需求表述不完整或语义偏差，因此需要详细的人类反馈。

Method: VLP框架包含三项核心技术：(i) 具有无歧义语法的NL风格文学化语言，以及代码到文档的确定性翻译；(ii) 基于LLM的细粒度不匹配检测，利用提示与文档之间的追踪链接聚焦用户审查；(iii) 验证模块，利用用户验证过的文档推导API使用检查和形式化属性，通过模型检查验证代码。

Result: VLP将代码pass@1从28.7%-73.2%提升至65.4%-93.5%，且用户工作量合理。

Conclusion: VLP通过将无歧义的自然语言文档作为人类可读的中间层，使得各级编程水平的用户都能参与LLM生成代码的审查与验证，有效弥合用户意图与代码实现之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Vibe coding democratizes software development by allowing users to generate code via natural-language (NL) interaction with large language models (LLMs). However, the code is reliable only when it faithfully implements the user's intent, which is difficult and labor-intensive for users to validate. Existing validation methods either rely on LLM-assisted automated testing, which suffers from prompt ambiguity and model fallibility, or involve users only in partial software artifacts such as prompts and test cases, which may overlook corner cases and program details. Motivated by a bug study of LLM-generated code, we find that detailed human feedback is essential, as failures often stem from underspecified requirements or subtle semantic deviations.
  This paper presents verifiable literate programming (VLP), a human-in-the-loop framework designed to make the review/validation process of LLM-generated code accessible to users at all programming levels. At its core, VLP proposes unambiguous NL-based documentation as a readable intermediate layer between prompts and code. The documentation demonstrates concrete program semantics and enables users to provide feedback on potential intent-code mismatches. It supports human-involved, end-to-end repair and validation via three techniques: (i) an NL-style literate language with unambiguous syntax and mostly deterministic code-to-documentation translation, (ii) LLM-based fine-grained mismatch detection that uses trace links between prompts and documentation to focus users' review effort on suspicious documentation lines, and (iii) a verification module that leverages user-validated documentation to derive API-usage checks and formal properties, which are then verified against the generated code using model checking. Our evaluation shows that VLP improves code pass@1 from 28.7%-73.2% to 65.4%-93.5% with reasonable user effort.

</details>


### [97] [Developers' Experience with Generative AI Beyond Productivity Assessment -- Insights from an Empirical Mixed-Methods Field Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02337)
*Charlotte Brandebusemeyer,Kerim Zunic,Thomas Zimmermann,Tobias Schimmer,Bert Arnrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法实证研究，调查了专业开发者在自然工作环境中使用生成式AI的体验，发现开发者对GenAI整体满意，尤其是在单调重复任务中；独立使用代码内建议或聊天提示能提升效率并降低认知负荷，但两者结合使用反而削弱收益；研究还提出基于任务特征选择交互类型的经验法则，并发现AI交互本身增加认知负荷，而感知生产力取决于AI输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究多聚焦于AI编码助手的输出质量和生产力提升，而忽视了开发者的幸福感与交互体验。本文旨在填补这一空白，从开发者中心视角出发，研究专业开发者在自然工作环境中与生成式AI的真实互动体验。

Method: 采用开发者中心的实证混合方法研究，结合受控数据采集会话和自然工作周期，在专业开发者的真实工作环境中进行调查。

Result: 1）开发者对GenAI整体满意，尤其在单调、重复和结构化任务中；2）独立使用代码内建议或聊天提示均能提升效率并降低感知工作负荷，但两者在同一任务中结合使用会削弱收益；3）开发密集型任务中，感知认知负荷源自AI交互本身，而感知生产力取决于AI输出质量；4）参与研究本身提升了开发者对GenAI工具的意识与有意使用。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在真实世界中采用混合方法研究设计对于理解GenAI工具及开发者体验的价值，并提出了基于任务特征选择交互类型的经验法则。

Abstract: With the growing adoption of AI-powered coding assistants, organizations and developers are increasingly seeking to optimize their interaction with these tools. Prior research has largely focused on output quality and productivity gains, with limited attention paid to developers' well-being and interaction experiences. This paper presents a developer-centered empirical mixed-methods study to investigate how professional developers engage with Generative AI (GenAI) in their natural work environment. Controlled data collection sessions are combined with natural work periods. Results show that developers are generally satisfied with GenAI, particularly for monotonous, repetitive, and structured tasks, and report perceived efficiency and productivity gains. Copilot interaction type preferences differ by task type and complexity: While both in-code suggestions and chat-based prompting independently improve task efficiency and reduce perceived workload, combining these interaction types within a single task diminishes benefits. We propose a rule-of-thumb for selecting an interaction type based on task characteristics. During development-heavy tasks, results indicate that perceived cognitive load arises from AI interaction, while perceived productivity depends on AI output quality. Participation in this study positively influenced developers' awareness and intentional use of GenAI tools. These findings demonstrate the value of real-world, mixed-methods study designs to understand GenAI tools and developers' experiences with them.

</details>


### [98] [SkillFuzz: Fuzzing Skill Composition for Implicit Intents Discovery in Open Skill Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02345)
*Jinwei Hu,Yi Dong,Youcheng Sun,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillFuzz，首个免执行的测试方法，通过结构化技能契约和蒙特卡洛树搜索，在技能组合中发现隐式意图（即多个良性技能组合后导致智能体偏离预期目标的问题）。实验证明该方法能高效发现1000+隐式意图，高威胁组合验证准确率超80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的技能市场对单个技能独立审计，无法检测技能组合后产生的隐式意图（implicit intents），即多个看似无害的技能组合后可能导致智能体偏离预期目标。检测难点在于：效果仅通过组合涌现、执行环境在准入时不可用、组合空间随市场规模指数级增长。

Method: 将隐式意图发现形式化为技能组合的模糊测试问题：技能组合为测试单元，规划产物在执行前暴露意图，无技能基线作为差异预言。提出SkillFuzz框架，提取结构化技能契约，利用契约引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）优先探索潜在冲突组合。

Result: 在代表性技能市场工作负载下，固定查询预算内发现1000+不同隐式意图；执行时验证中80%以上高风险组合被确认；相比替代搜索策略，探索极少量交互空间即可识别更多高严重性隐式意图。

Conclusion: SkillFuzz有效解决了技能市场中组合式隐式意图的检测难题，是首个免执行的技能组合安全测试方法。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents increasingly automate software engineering tasks through reusable skills, natural-language instruction documents that guide planning and execution. Open skill marketplaces enable users to assemble agents by co-activating community-contributed skills, but marketplace operators typically audit skills in isolation. As a result, individually benign skills may interact to redirect an agent toward unintended objectives, which we term implicit intents. Detecting such intents is challenging because the effect emerges only through skill composition, execution environments are often unavailable at admission time, and the space of possible co-activations grows exponentially with marketplace size. In this paper, we formulate implicit-intent discovery as a fuzzing problem over skill compositions, where skill compositions are the unit under test, planning artifacts expose agent intent before execution, and deviations from a skill-free baseline serve as a differential oracle. Based on this formulation, we propose skillfuzz, the first execution-free testing approach that extracts structured skill contracts and uses contract-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search to prioritize potentially conflicting compositions. Across representative skill-marketplace workloads, skillfuzz discovers over 1,000 distinct implicit intents under a fixed query budget, confirms more than 80% of the highest-risk flagged compositions during execution-time validation, and identifies substantially more high-severity implicit intents than alternative search strategies while exploring only a fraction of the pairwise interaction space they require.

</details>


### [99] [Understanding Agent-Based Patching of Compiler Missed Optimizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02370)
*Batu Guan,Zirui Wang,Shaohua Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了智能体在修补编译器遗漏优化问题上的表现，发现核心挑战在于补丁需要泛化到相似案例而不仅仅是修复单个案例。通过构建LLVM遗漏优化基准测试，对比智能体与开发者的补丁在优化范围上的差异，并提出了基于历史知识的增强技术来提升补丁的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编译器遗漏优化是常见问题，需要开发者投入大量精力去实现或修补。但目前缺乏对智能体在这类任务上表现的系统研究，特别是补丁需要泛化到类似案例这一关键挑战尚未被充分探讨。

Method: 构建了一个真实世界的LLVM遗漏优化问题基准测试集，对比了智能体生成的补丁与开发者补丁在优化范围上的差异。同时提出了历史知识增强技术，通过检索和蒸馏方式利用先前的LLVM优化拉取请求来改善补丁的泛化能力。

Result: 智能体通常能优化给定的示例，但生成的补丁要么只覆盖了开发者意图的一部分，要么与之部分重叠，有些情况下甚至会超出参考补丁的泛化范围。引入历史知识增强技术后，补丁与开发者意图的一致性得到改善。

Conclusion: 智能体修补编译器遗漏优化面临泛化挑战，历史知识增强技术能够有效提升补丁的泛化能力，并在真实IR上产生实用价值。

Abstract: Compiler missed optimizations refer to cases in which compilers failed to optimize certain code. It takes many compiler developers' efforts to implement or patch such missed optimizations. In this paper, we present a systematic study of how well agents patch compiler missed optimizations. We identify a significant challenge that patching a missed optimization requires more than just fixing the reported case, and instead requires generalizing to similar cases. We construct a benchmark of real-world LLVM missed optimization issues and compare agent-generated patches with patches from developers in terms of optimization scope. Our results show that coding agents often optimize the given examples, but many generated patches either cover only part of the developer-intended scope or partially overlap with it; in some cases, they further generalize beyond the reference patch. We further introduce historical-knowledge augmentation techniques that leverage prior LLVM optimization pull requests through retrieval and distillation, showing that they improve developer-aligned generalization and yield practical benefits when applied to real-world IR.

</details>


### [100] [Reasoning effort, not tool access, buys first-try reliability in agentic code generation: an observational study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02436)
*Achint Mehta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过90次独立智能体运行，测试了"更多能力=更好软件"的假设。结果表明：模型能力层级主导性能（前沿模型接近满分，本地模型仅24-37分）；容器部署是最主要缺陷（44%首次失败）；测试工具增加42-68%成本却未提升功能或可靠性；将推理努力从High提升至xHigh使首次完美运行从28%升至89%；设计导向提示提升了视觉质量（4.5 vs 3.0/5分）但未改善功能。核心启示：多数首次失败源于推理不足，而非可被检查工具捕获的可见缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证"赋予智能体编码助手更多能力（如浏览器测试工具、设计导向系统提示）必然带来更好软件"这一普遍假设是否成立。

Method: 设计90次独立智能体运行，构建同一实时回溯板应用，使用固定14标准功能评分表（42分满分）和视觉质量评审。跨越多个模型世代、两种智能体框架、两种推理努力水平、一个测试工具和两种设计导向提示进行对比实验。

Result: 前沿模型趋近满分，本地模型仅24-37分；容器部署首次失败率44%；测试工具使成本增加42-68%但功能/可靠性无改善；xHigh推理努力使首次完美运行从28%升至89%，纠正提示减少约5倍，成本增加9-29%；设计提示提升视觉质量（4.5 vs 3.0）但未改善功能。

Conclusion: 多数首次运行失败源于推理能力不足（可通过更强模型或更高推理努力预防），而非可被检查工具捕获的可见缺陷。应匹配修复方案与失败根源：提升推理能力比增加检查工具更有效。

Abstract: Agentic coding assistants are increasingly given extra capabilities, such as browser based testing tools and design oriented system prompts, on the assumption that more capability yields better software. This study tested that assumption directly. Ninety independent agent runs built the same application, a real time retrospective board, from one detailed specification, each scored on a fixed 14 criterion functional rubric (42 point maximum) and a visual quality review. The runs spanned several model generations, two agent harnesses, two reasoning effort levels, a testing tool, and two design oriented prompts. Capability tier dominated: frontier models clustered near the ceiling while a low cost local model fell to 24 to 37 points. A criterion level analysis revealed what run totals conceal. Container deployment was the dominant defect, failing first try in 44 percent of runs, with its failure rate shifting sharply across model generations while mean totals moved less than a point. The testing tool raised cost by 42 to 68 percent without improving functional score or reliability, even on interface visible criteria. Raising reasoning effort from High to xHigh lifted first try perfect runs from 28 percent to 89 percent and cut corrective prompts about five fold, for 9 to 29 percent more cost. A design oriented prompt raised visual quality, 4.5 versus 3.0 on a 5 point scale, without lifting function, and a one paragraph paraphrase of its directive reproduced the entire lift. The practical lesson is to match the fix to the failure: most first run failures came from weak reasoning, which a stronger model or more effort prevents, not from visible flaws a checking tool would catch.

</details>


### [101] [TestEvo-Bench: An Executable and Live Benchmark for Test and Code Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02469)
*Jiale Amber Wang,Kaiyuan Wang,Pengyu Nie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TestEvo-Bench 是一个测试与代码协同演化基准，包含746个测试生成和509个测试更新任务，来自152个开源Java项目。该基准支持可执行性验证、实时更新以降低数据泄露风险。实验表明，最先进智能体在测试生成和更新任务上分别达到77.5%和74.6%的成功率，但在最新任务和有限成本下性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试生成和更新基准常将测试与代码变更分离，依赖静态元数据，无法验证测试是否可执行或与代码变更语义绑定，难以评估测试自动化智能体是否理解代码变更应如何传播到测试套件中。

Method: 从软件仓库中挖掘测试与代码协同演化任务，构建两个赛道：测试生成（为新行为编写新测试）和测试更新（调整失效测试以适应变更）。每个任务锚定真实提交历史并打包环境配置，支持执行指标（通过率、覆盖率、变异分数）。采用动态基准设计，记录时间戳并定期挖掘新任务，支持对模型训练截止日期后的任务进行评估以降低数据泄露风险。当前快照从152个开源Java项目的59,950条候选记录中筛选出746个测试生成和509个测试更新任务。

Result: 在四种最先进智能体（Claude Code、Gemini CLI、SWE-Agent结合Claude Opus 4.7和Gemini 3.1 Pro）上，测试生成成功率最高达77.5%，测试更新成功率最高达74.6%。但在最新基准任务上成功率显著降低，且每任务成本受限时性能大幅下降。

Conclusion: TestEvo-Bench 通过执行可验证的协同演化任务和动态基准设计，为评估测试自动化智能体提供了更可靠的评测框架。当前模型虽表现尚可，但在最新任务和资源受限场景下仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Software tests and code evolve together: a code change should be followed by new or updated tests that record the new software behavior. Yet existing test generation and update benchmarks often isolate the test from the code change, and rely on static metadata that does not verify whether a test is executable or semantically tied to the code change. This makes it difficult to evaluate whether a test automation agent understands how a code change should propagate into the test suite.
  We introduce TestEvo-Bench, a benchmark of test and code co-evolution tasks mined from software repositories, with two tracks: in test generation, the agent shall write new tests to capture the new software behavior; in test update, the agent shall adapt failing existing tests to the changed software behavior. Each task is anchored to a real commit history and packaged with environment configuration to support execution-grounded metrics such as pass rate, coverage, and mutation score. TestEvo-Bench is also a live benchmark: each task records the timestamp of the test and code changes, and new tasks are periodically mined by our automated pipeline, so evaluation can be restricted to tasks postdating a model's training cutoff to reduce data leakage risk. The current snapshot contains 746 test generation and 509 test update tasks, curated from 59,950 candidate co-evolution records across 152 open-source Java projects. We experiment with four state-of-the-art agents that combine strong harnesses (Claude Code, Gemini CLI, and SWE-Agent) with strong foundation models (Claude Opus 4.7 and Gemini 3.1 Pro). Results show that they achieve up to 77.5% success rate on test generation and 74.6% on test update. However, success rate is materially lower on the most recent benchmark tasks and drops significantly under limited per-task cost.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [ExPerT: Personalizing LLM Responses to Users' Domain Expertise via Query-Wise Semantic and Keystroke Behavioral Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01242)
*Yeji Park,Jiwon Tark,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ExPerT是一个查询级个性化框架，通过结合语义和键盘行为线索推断用户领域专业知识，并据此调整LLM响应的细节、术语和复杂度，在40人1270次查询实验中，专业知识推断误差降低65.7%，响应满意度提升17.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化方法依赖静态档案或纯文本信号，无法捕获用户在不同查询上的专业知识差异，导致响应与用户实际水平不匹配。

Method: 提出ExPerT框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 语义-行为专业知识推断模块，通过上下文LLM提示联合解析查询文本和键盘动力学特征；(2) 专业知识条件响应生成，根据推断的专业水平调整响应细节、术语和概念复杂度。

Result: 在40名参与者、1270个查询的用户研究中，ExPerT的专业知识推断误差（MAE=0.398）相比最强基线（MAE=1.162）降低65.7%，响应满意度从3.71提升至4.36（5点Likert量表），提升17.52%。

Conclusion: 结合语义和行为线索的查询级专业知识推断能有效提升LLM响应的个性化质量，未来可探索更多行为信号和更广泛的部署场景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used by end users, yet existing personalization methods relying on static profiles or text-only signals fail to capture query-specific expertise variation. We present ExPerT, a query-wise personalization framework that adapts LLM responses to users' query domain expertise by combining semantic and behavioral cues. ExPerT consists of two key components: (i) a semantic-behavioral expertise inference module that jointly interprets query text and keystroke dynamics via in-context LLM prompting, and (ii) an expertise-conditioned response generation that adapts the level of detail, terminology, and conceptual complexity. Our user study with 40 participants and 1270 queries demonstrated that ExPerT reduced expertise inference error by 65.7% compared to the strongest baseline (MAE = 0.398 vs. 1.162) and improved response satisfaction by 17.52% (from 3.71 to 4.36) on a 5-point Likert scale.

</details>


### [103] [Mitigating Confirmation Bias through Hand-Drawing Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01359)
*Chenyu Lin,Cindy Xiong,Icy Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究是否可以通过展示可视化图表的动态手绘过程（而非静态图表）来减轻确认偏差，提高数据解读准确性。实验结果表明，手绘视频帮助参与者更准确地解读数据，即使数据与他们的先前信念相冲突，并减少了信念一致的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据可视化解读常受先验信念影响甚至扭曲，导致确认偏差。现有方法多聚焦于设计静态图表来缓解偏差，但效果有限。作者探索是否可以通过揭示可视化的构建过程（动态手绘）来促进更客观的推理。

Method: 开展对照实验，比较静态条形图与手绘构建过程视频两种呈现方式。实验设置与参与者先验信念一致或冲突的两种数据情境，测量解读准确性、信念一致错误和信念覆盖响应等指标。

Result: 手绘视频组在数据与先验信念冲突时仍能准确解读数据；减少了信念一致性错误；增加了信念覆盖响应（即参与者根据数据修正自己的判断）。

Conclusion: 暴露可视化的构建过程有助于更准确的推理，减轻确认偏差的影响。该工作为设计偏差缓解的数据界面提供了新的设计空间。

Abstract: Understanding data visualizations is essential for informed decision-making, yet interpretation is often shaped and even distorted by prior beliefs. We investigate whether an embodied pedagogical approach, in which viewers observe the dynamic hand-drawing of a visualization, can mitigate confirmation bias and improve interpretation accuracy. We conducted a study comparing static bar charts to videos in which charts are constructed through hand-drawing, across contexts that either align with or challenge participants' prior beliefs. The results indicate that hand-drawn videos helped participants accurately interpret data, even when the data conflicted with their prior beliefs. This approach also reduced belief-consistent errors and increased belief-overriding responses. These findings suggest that exposing the construction process of a visualization supports more accurate reasoning and mitigates the influence of confirmation bias. Consequently, this work introduces a promising design space for bias-mitigating data interfaces.

</details>


### [104] [Mind the Trust Gap: Identifying (Mis)alignments in Teacher-Student Views Toward Control and Agency in K-12 Classroom AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01506)
*Tomohiro Nagashima,Lisa Siegrist,Niklas Scholz,Shintaro Sato,Martina Vincoli,Man Su*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对16名德国学生和15名德国教师进行故事板速配研究，发现师生在AI决策控制、信任程度以及社会情感学习等方面存在观点错位，并探讨了填补视角差距的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-12课堂中引入AI技术涉及多个利益相关者，但先前研究未充分揭示教师与学生之间对AI使用看法的对齐或错位情况。

Method: 采用故事板速配研究（speed-dating study），对16名K-12学生和15名教师进行配对分析，探究师生在学生-AI决策控制方面的看法对齐与错位。

Result: 师生在AI信任程度、社会情感学习等关键主题上存在观点错位；师生关系（超越AI使用）对利益相关者观点和互动具有重要影响。

Conclusion: 师生-AI交互中的偏好具有复杂性，取决于利益相关者之间的动态关系；需要策略来填补师生之间的视角差距。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based technologies have been integrated into school classrooms where multiple stakeholders (with different roles) interact with each other, it is critical to deeply understand stakeholder views in the classroom. In particular, prior work has not fully uncovered how teachers' and school students' views might or might not align well with each other, especially in K-12 classrooms. We conducted a speed-dating study using storyboards with 16 school students and 15 school teachers in Germany to investigate alignments and misalignments between their views on student-AI decision-making control in K-12 classroom. Through an explicit pair-matching analysis, we found that students and teachers had misaligned views on several key topics, including how much they trust AI and social and emotional aspects of student learning with AI. Findings also revealed the importance of teacher-student relationships outside of AI use that shape stakeholders' views and interactions. We discuss potential reasons for the observed misaligned views and strategies to fill the perspective gaps. This study illustrates the complexities of preferences in teacher-student-AI interactions that depend on the dynamic relations among the stakeholders.

</details>


### [105] [OrchestrXR: A Multi-Agent System for Idea-to-Prototype XR Study Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01588)
*Shuqi Liao,Chenfei Zhu,Karthik Ramani,Voicu Popescu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: OrchestrXR 是一个多智能体人机协作工作流，旨在将早期 XR 研究想法转化为可运行的 Unity 原型，支持研究设计、场景生成和交互生成的端到端可控流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR 研究从想法到可运行原型的转化过程碎片化严重，涉及研究设计、场景构建和交互实现等多个环节，缺乏统一的工具支持。

Method: 提出 OrchestrXR，一个多智能体人机协作工作流，通过结构化模式（schemas）、多智能体编排（multi-agent orchestration）和交互式人机界面，将研究想法分阶段转化为 Unity 原型。

Result: 12 名 XR 研究者的用户研究表明，OrchestrXR 能为早期 XR 研究创作提供有效支持，并在各阶段保持较强的意图一致性。

Conclusion: OrchestrXR 实现了从想法到原型的可控工作流，解决了 XR 研究创作中跨阶段的碎片化问题，并有效保留了研究者的原始意图。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) has become an important interaction paradigm in Human-Computer Interaction (HCI). XR studies are used to investigate interaction, perception, and user behavior in immersive environments, and typically involve experimental tasks, 3D scenes, and interactive logic.
