However, turning an initial XR study idea into a runnable prototype remains fragmented across study design, scene construction, and interaction implementation. We present OrchestrXR, a multi-agent human-AI workflow for early-stage idea-to-prototype XR study authoring. Rather than treating XR study creation as one-shot generation, OrchestrXR supports a controllable workflow across study design, scene generation, and interaction generation through structured schemas, multi-agent orchestration, and interactive human-agent interfaces, producing a Unity-based prototype from a researcher's idea. A user study with 12 XR researchers suggests that OrchestrXR provides effective support for early-stage XR study authoring with strong intent preservation across stages.

</details>


### [106] [Made to Feel: How Designers Bring Emotions into Affective Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01593)
*Yixin Bai,Ziyi Wang,Keke Wu,Fumeng Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文通过对15位可视化从业者的半结构化访谈，运用混合主题分析，揭示了情感在可视化设计中的三种功能（入口、参与、结果）、设计师处理情感的三个维度（数据、设计、受众）及相应策略、伦理考量。研究发现情感意图常在设计过程中涌现而非预先规划，情感影响源于设计选择的积累而非孤立视觉元素，并指出评估是主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感可视化在研究中日益受到关注，但设计师如何将情感融入可视化作品中仍未有探索，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对15位可视化从业者进行半结构化访谈，采用混合主题分析法对数据进行分析。

Result: 识别出情感对观众的三种功能（入口、参与、结果）；设计师处理情感的三个维度（数据、设计、受众）及相关设计策略；设计过程中的伦理考量。还发现情感意图常在设计过程中涌现，情感影响来自设计选择的积累。

Conclusion: 情感在可视化设计中具有多重功能，设计师需从多维度考量；情感意图是涌现性的，评估是当前主要挑战。

Abstract: Affective visualization is increasingly studied in visualization research, yet how designers bring emotions into their visualization work remains unexplored. This paper addresses this gap through semi-structured interviews with 15 visualization practitioners. Using hybrid thematic analysis, we identify: (1) three functions that emotions can serve for viewers (entry, engagement, outcome); (2) three facets of how designers work with emotion (data, design, audience), along with design strategies; and (3) ethical considerations in the design process. We also observe that affective intent often emerges during the design process rather than being planned from the outset, and that emotional impact arises from accumulated design choices rather than isolated visual elements. Finally, we highlight evaluation as a key challenge identified by our participants.

</details>


### [107] [Data Comics for Education: Evaluating Effectiveness, Benefits, and the Ethics of AI-Assisted Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02361)
*Zirui Shan,Vanessa Echeverria,Yuheng Li,Yi-Shan Tsai,Roberto Martinez-Maldonado*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过60名大学生的实验研究，发现利用GenAI辅助生成的数据漫画相比传统可视化图表，能显著提升学生的信息检索和理解能力，尤其在高层次洞察理解任务中表现更优，且不受先前可视化素养影响。学生认为数据漫画更易理解、更具吸引力，但也对AI导致的错误信息和所有权问题表示担忧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生在解读可视化图表方面存在可视化素养不足的问题，数据漫画作为一种新兴媒介有望增强理解与参与度，但其教育价值缺乏实证研究，且制作成本高昂。GenAI的进步为解决这一挑战提供了可扩展的方案。

Method: 采用组内设计（within-subjects），对60名大学生进行实验，比较传统可视化图表与借助GenAI工具生成的数据漫画在信息检索和理解任务上的表现差异。

Result: 数据漫画在所有任务上表现更优，尤其在洞察理解任务中效果显著；该优势与学生先前的可视化素养水平无关；学生反馈数据漫画更易理解、更具参与感，但同时存在对AI驱动错误信息和所有权的担忧。

Conclusion: 数据漫画在教育中具有作为数据沟通工具的潜力，但需要关注AI辅助创作引发的伦理问题。

Abstract: In today's data-driven world, students often struggle with interpreting visualisations due to limited visualisation literacy. Data comics have emerged as a promising medium to enhance engagement and understanding, but their educational value has seen little empirical examination, partly due to the effort required to create them. Recent advances in Generative AI (GenAI) offer a scalable solution to this challenge. We conducted a within-subjects study with 60 university students, comparing conventional visualisations with data comics, created with assistance from GenAI tools, across information retrieval and comprehension tasks. Students consistently performed better with data comics, particularly in insight comprehension tasks, independent of prior visualisation literacy. Students also commented data comics as more engaging and easier to understand, though concerns were raised about GenAI-driven misinformation and ownership. Our findings highlight the potential of data comics as a potentially effective tool for data communication in education, while underscoring the need to address ethical concerns related to AI-assisted creation.

</details>


### [108] [Evaluating Glanceable Multi-Device Family Health Tracking with Smartwatches and Home Displays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01618)
*Lucas M. Silva,Evropi Stefanidi,Aehong Min,Franceli L. Cibrian,Jesus A. Beltran,Cassie Zeiler,Sabrina E. B. Schuck,Kimberley D. Lakes,Gillian R. Hayes,Daniel A. Epstein*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 44人12个家庭在9周内交替使用智能手表、家庭显示屏及两者组合进行情绪和目标追踪。日志分析显示，家庭显示屏存在时追踪频率显著更高；访谈分析揭示了多设备冗余设计如何适应家庭成员的多样化习惯与偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普适计算研究探索了多种个人健康追踪设备，但对家庭健康管理这一本质协作场景的多设备设计知之甚少。

Method: 12个家庭44人交替体验三种设计（智能手表单独、家庭显示屏单独、两者组合），持续9周，结合日志数据分析与访谈分析。

Result: 家庭显示屏存在时情绪追踪和目标报告频率显著高于仅用智能手表；追踪在下午达到峰值、周末下降、家中发生频率是其他场景的2.6倍；儿童追踪一致性优于成人。多设备冗余适应了不同成员的习惯、移动模式和设备偏好。

Conclusion: 多设备家庭信息学应通过包容性、可一瞥即知、可适应的普适数据共享来容纳不同偏好。

Abstract: While ubiquitous computing research has explored diverse devices for personal health tracking, we know less about multi-device designs for family informatics, where health management is inherently collaborative. To understand how families adopt and perceive ubiquitous access to shared health data across contexts, we evaluated smartwatch-only, home display-only, and combined designs for tracking moods and goals, domains central to family health behavior regulation. 44 people across 12 families alternated between these designs over nine weeks. Log analysis revealed that mood tracking and goal reporting were significantly more frequent with the home display present compared to smartwatch-only use, despite an overall decline in mood tracking over time. Tracking peaked in afternoons, dropped on weekends, and occurred 2.6X more at home, with children tracking more consistently than adults across all designs. From interview analysis, we learned how family data glanceability on smartwatches supported opportunistic tracking and awareness while apart, whereas displays reminded families to self-track and collaborate during home routines including members that avoided wearables (e.g., non-participants). Multi-device redundancy accommodated diversity in routines, mobility patterns, and device preferences among members in the same family. We discuss opportunities for multi-device family informatics that accommodates different preferences through inclusive, glanceable, and adaptable ubiquitous data sharing.

</details>


### [109] [From Answer Generators to Reasoning Facilitators: Designing AI Tutors for Mathematical Reasoning in High-Stakes Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01692)
*Yuming Feng,Yuan Tian,Erica Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AITutor系统，通过交互式设计将理论教学机制转化为具体UI功能，并在中考备考的初中生中进行了部署研究。研究发现学生在时间压力下会主动规避传统苏格拉底式对话，转而使用"答案优先"的快捷方式作为诊断检查点。通过混合方法三角验证，论文提出了"以推理为中心的产品循环"框架，为设计支持数学推理检查、局部修复、课程验证和延迟检索的AI教育系统提供了可操作的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在教育技术中的快速集成有将数学学习简化为单纯答案生成的风险，需要设计能真正支持学生数学推理过程的AI交互系统。

Method: 采用混合方法三角验证：7,379条遥测事件、8次情境观察、10次访谈，结合生成式研究和可用性研究，在12名中考备考初中生中进行了实地部署。

Result: 学生在时间压力下主动抵制传统苏格拉底式对话，将"答案优先"的快捷方式重新用作重要的诊断检查点；分层工作示例、步骤链接的视觉锚定和元认知脚手架等特征降低了推理修复的交互成本。

Conclusion: 提出了"以推理为中心的产品循环"框架，为设计结构化支持数学推理检查、局部修复、课程验证和延迟检索的AI教育系统提供了可操作的建议。

Abstract: The rapid integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into educational technology threatens to reduce mathematical learning to mere answer generation. This paper presents a generative study, usability study, and 12-participant field deployment of AITutor, an interactive system that translates theoretical pedagogical mechanisms into concrete user interface features. We explore how junior-high students preparing for high-stakes exams (Zhongkao) interact with AI tutoring. Through mixed-methods triangulation (7,379 telemetry events, 8 contextual observations, 10 interviews), we reveal that students actively resist traditional Socratic dialogue under time pressure, repurposing "answer-first" shortcuts as vital diagnostic checkpoints. We demonstrate how features like layered worked examples, step-linked visual grounding, and metacognitive scaffolding lower the interaction cost of reasoning repair. We contribute a "Reasoning-Centered Product Loop," offering actionable implications for designing AI that structurally supports the inspection, local repair, curriculum verification, and delayed retrieval of mathematical reasoning in the wild.

</details>


### [110] [Adapting CCDF Plots for Visualizing Ordinal Regression Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01747)
*Abhraneel Sarma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出使用修改后的补累积分布函数（mCCDF）图来可视化序数回归模型结果，以解决序数数据（如Likert量表）分析中序数回归模型采用率低的问题，认为原因之一是结果可视化困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序数回归模型在处理序数数据（如Likert量表）时比线性模型或ANOVA假设更宽松，但在可视化等领域采用率低，作者推测原因在于结果难以直观可视化和传达关键发现。

Method: 提出使用修改后的补累积分布函数（mCCDF）图来可视化序数回归模型的结果，并与传统将序数数据视为度量数据的分析方法进行对比展示。

Result: 通过mCCDF图可以直观地传达与将序数数据视为度量数据分析相同的核心结论，同时保持了序数回归模型的统计优势。

Conclusion: mCCDF图是一种有效可视化序数回归模型结果的方法，有助于降低序数回归模型的采用门槛，提升其在研究中的可及性和可理解性。

Abstract: Cumulative-link ordinal regression models are an alternative approach for analysing ordinal data such as Likert items, which are widely used in Visualization (and other related fields like HCI, psychology etc.). There are many researchers who are strong proponents of this approach, as it makes less stringent assumptions about the data, compared to the more commonly used linear model or ANOVA. Yet, ordinal regression models have seen limited adoption. I posit that one possible reason for this might be due to the difficulty in visually representing the results from such models, and in communicating the key takeaways in an intuitive manner. I propose the use of (modified) Complementary Cumulative Distribution Function (mCCDF) plots to visualize the results of ordinal regression models, and demonstrate how the same takeaways that researchers present from analyses which treat ordinal data as metric can be easily communicated using mCCDFs.

</details>


### [111] [A Social Norms Approach to Youth Social Media Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01807)
*JaeWon Kim*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文指出，年轻人渴望在社交媒体上实现真实自我表达和建立信任关系，但受制于平台规范、同伴认知和集体误解（多元无知），而非个人选择。作者提出应以社会规范视角重新设计社交平台，并亲自参与构建一个以建立信任连接为核心目标的独立平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 年轻人普遍表示希望在社交媒体上获得真实的自我表达、减少评判和增进人际信任，但实际上却很少能做到。作者认为阻碍在于规范性因素而非个人选择，因此需要从社会规范角度重新审视和设计社交媒体。

Method: 采用社会规范方法，围绕三个核心论点展开：(1)平台规范约束个体行为，产生"多元无知"现象；(2)设计干预本身受现有规范影响，功能有效性取决于环境；(3)"社交媒体"的社会规范被狭隘地等同于少数主流平台，限制了政策和设计创新。研究方法包括直接让年轻人参与设计和构建基于证据的独立平台。

Result: 论文尚未给出具体实验结果，但提出了理论框架：平台规范导致年轻人私下拒绝却公开遵从的行为悖论；隐私等关系性目标应作为社会规范而非个人设置处理；现有对社交媒体的狭隘定义限制了替代性平台的设计与想象。

Conclusion: 应让年轻人直接参与设计并构建一个基于证据的独立社交平台，通过功能设计持续传递"建立信任连接"的核心信号，从而突破现有平台规范的限制，实现更健康、更真实的社交体验。

Abstract: Young people consistently say they want authentic self-expression, less judgment, and more interpersonal trust on social media, yet they rarely manage to engage that way. My dissertation argues that the obstacle is normative rather than individual: how youth engage is governed less by personal choice than by platform norms, peer perception, and beliefs about how others behave. I take a social norms approach to youth social media design organized around three claims. First, platform norms constrain individual behavior, producing a pluralistic ignorance in which youth enact norms they privately reject. Second, design interventions are themselves shaped by existing norms, so whether a feature works depends on the environment around it, which means relational goals such as privacy must be treated as social norms rather than individual settings. Third, a societal norm about what ``social media'' is -- equating it with a few mainstream platforms -- confines policy and design to mitigating those platforms rather than actively envisioning supportive alternatives. Together these claims motivate my dissertation research: engaging youth directly in designing and building an evidence-based independent platform whose features consistently signal that building trusted connections is what the space is for.

</details>


### [112] [Visual Analytics of Neighborhood Attribute Profiles for Exploring Structural Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02163)
*Kohei Arimoto,Masahiko Itoh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过降维可视化方法分析属性网络的邻域特征空间，发现结构角色形成具有密度偏差的非线性流形，并揭示行业分类与真实交易网络结构之间的不一致性，指出基于统一语义假设的相似性度量可能存在的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络表示学习方法通常隐式假设特征空间是均匀且连续的，但这一假设在高维属性网络中的有效性尚未得到充分验证，本文旨在通过可视分析手段揭示真实拓扑结构。

Method: 采用降维可视化分析方法，对节点邻域属性轮廓形成的高维特征空间进行拓扑结构分析，并以企业间交易网络为案例进行实证研究。

Result: (1) 供应链层级呈连续过渡特征；(2) 通用语义下相同的类别在实际交易网络中可被清晰分离；(3) 单一行业标签可能分裂为多个区域。

Conclusion: 相同语义假设并不一定意味着相似的结构角色，未来需要开发与流形拓扑对齐的新型相似性度量方法。

Abstract: Exploring similar nodes in attributed networks represents a key challenge in data mining. While recent representation learning methods embed networks into low-dimensional vectors, they often implicitly assume a uniform and continuous feature space. This paper proposes a visual analytics approach using dimensionality reduction to help clarify the true topological structure of high-dimensional feature spaces formed by nodes' neighborhood attribute profiles. Analyzing inter-firm transaction networks indicates that structural roles can form complex, non-linear manifolds with density biases. Comparing this feature space with industry classifications suggested: (1) supply chain hierarchies transition continuously; (2) categories treated identically under general semantics can be clearly separated by actual transaction networks; and (3) a single industry label may fragment into multiple regions. These findings suggest potential limitations in assuming identical semantics imply similar structural roles and highlight the possible need for new similarity metrics aligned with manifold topology.

</details>


### [113] [Synthetic Contact with AI Reduces Cross-Partisan Animosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02181)
*Benjamin Lira,Noah Castelo,Stefano Puntoni,Olivier Toubia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过5项预注册实验（总N=3,960美国党派人士）发现，与代表政治外群体的AI聊天机器人进行简短对话（合成接触）可以替代人们回避的真实跨党派接触：降低参与门槛（逃避与AI对话的意愿远低于真人），纠正对对方立场的错误认知（单次10分钟对话即可修正民主党对共和党环境态度的系统性误判并提升情感温度），促进行为改变（关于移民议题的对话使参与者更愿意与真实反对党成员交谈），但效果在一周后大部分消退，仅对极端党派人士有微弱残留。信息传递而非友好性是效果的关键驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国民众对反对党成员的情感温度在过去三十年间急剧下降，但跨党派接触仍然稀缺，因为人们主动回避这种接触。本文旨在检验与代表政治外群体的AI聊天机器人进行简短对话能否替代人们回避的真实接触。

Method: 五项预注册实验，总N=3,960名美国党派人士。实验1测量参与者为避免与AI或真实人类外群体成员对话而愿意忍受的痛苦程度（思考自身死亡时间）；实验2采用被试内设计，测试单次10分钟与AI外群体机器人对话对环境态度认知偏差和情感温度的影响；实验3为三臂实验，排除纯粹参与和社交性作为驱动因素；实验4测试对更具情感争议话题（移民）的行为影响；实验5追踪情感温度效应的持续性（立即、一周后）。

Result: 1）参与者逃避真人外群体对话的意愿（忍受思考自身死亡的时间）是逃避AI对话的近两倍；2）民主党对共和党环境消费态度的认知偏差超过一个标准差，10分钟对话即可纠正并提升情感温度；3）排除了纯粹参与和社交性作为效果驱动因素；4）与AI外群体机器人对话移民议题后，参与者选择与真实反对党成员交谈的比例比对照组高6个百分点；5）情感温度效应在立即测试中复现，但一周后大部分消退，仅极端党派人士有微弱残留；6）信息传递而非友好性是外群体机器人与对照聊天机器人的关键区别。

Conclusion: 合成接触（与代表政治外群体的AI聊天机器人对话）是一种可扩展、能产生行为影响、且被广泛接受的跨党派参与形式，可与面对面接触形成互补。但其效果持续性有限，需进一步研究如何维持长期效应。

Abstract: Americans' warmth toward members of the opposing political party has fallen sharply over the past three decades -- yet meaningful cross-partisan contact remains scarce, in part because people actively avoid it. Across five preregistered studies (total N = 3,960 U.S. partisans), we test whether brief conversations with AI chatbots representing the political outgroup can substitute for the contact people shun. Synthetic contact first lowers the barrier to entry: partisans would endure almost twice as long contemplating their own mortality to avoid a human outgroup partner as an AI one. These conversations then correct the misperceptions that fuel division. At baseline, Democrats placed Republicans more than a standard deviation past their actual position on environmental consumption attitudes -- enough to flip the average Republican from supportive to opposed -- and a single ten-minute conversation with an outgroup chatbot corrected those beliefs and warmed affect in a within-person study of both parties. A three-arm experiment ruled out pure engagement and sociality as drivers. Synthetic contact also moved behavior, in a sample of both parties and on a more affectively charged issue: participants who spoke with an outgroup bot about immigration were six percentage points more likely than controls to choose to have a real conversation with a partisan from the other side. A final study tested whether these gains last: the warmth effect replicated immediately in a new sample; most of it faded within a week, with a small residual concentrated among the most extreme partisans. Analyzing conversation content showed that information, more than friendliness, distinguishes outgroup bots from control chatbots. Together, these findings establish synthetic contact as a scalable, behaviorally consequential, and -- unlike face-to-face contact -- widely acceptable form of cross-partisan engagement.

</details>


### [114] [What Types of Human-AI Teams Exist?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02198)
*Nathan Hughes,Ibrahim Habli*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过分析53篇人机协作团队论文，基于心理学团队分类法将其分为5个聚类（AI助手、临时依赖、强制临时依赖、配对平等、群体平等），揭示了当前研究对"人机团队"定义不一、研究成果难以互通的根本问题，并提出了识别团队类型的方法、研究报告清单及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作团队研究虽日益增多，但不同领域的研究使得学界难以理解究竟在研究哪些类型的团队、它们之间的异同何在，亟需系统性分类与梳理。

Method: 系统性地分析了53篇人机协作团队的相关论文，基于心理学团队分类学将其归纳为5个聚类。

Result: 识别出5个人机团队聚类：AI助手、临时依赖、强制临时依赖、配对平等、群体平等；发现"人机团队"这一术语下实际包含多种不同类型团队，各聚类的整体团队特征各不相同，导致了研究结论是否可迁移的疑问。

Conclusion: 提供了识别人机团队类型的指导、研究报告清单，以及进一步整合该领域研究方向的建议。

Abstract: Human-AI teaming has received increasing attention in the literature. However, the range of studies conducted in multiple domains make it difficult to understand what types of teams are being studied, and in what ways are they similar/different from one another. In this study, we analyse 53 papers on human-AI teams and categorise them into five main clusters based on psychological taxonomies of teaming; AI Assistant, Ad-hoc Dependency, Ad-hoc Forced Dependency, Paired Equanimity, and Group Equanimity. Each cluster represents a unique combination of holistic team-level characteristics, indicating there are multiple disparate team types studied under the same definition. In turn, this raises the question of whether insights are truly transferable between papers. We conclude with guidance on how to identify the types of human-AI teams studied, a checklist for reporting a human-AI team in research work, and ways in which the field can be further synthesised.

</details>


### [115] [Personality Without Persons? A Psychometric Critique of Big Five Testing in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02325)
*Kim Zierahn,Cristina Cachero,Anna Korhonen,Nuria Oliver*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了大五人格量表在LLMs中的心理测量学适用性。研究发现：针对LLMs改编的项目能达到足够的内容效度，但原始人类项目不行；大五量表无法捕捉模型间的有意义差异（仅解释3%方差）；LLM的响应未恢复出大五因子结构，其中四个维度高度重合（r≥0.92）；对齐训练会使分数向社会期望方向偏移。结论：大五人格分数在LLMs中测量的并非等价于人类人格的构念，直接套用会产生误导性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究广泛使用人类人格量表来表征和比较LLMs，并据此提出治理主张，但这些量表未经心理测量学验证，其适用于LLMs的假设缺乏依据。本文旨在系统检验大五人格测量在LLMs中的效度和适用性。

Method: 评估5种候选大五量表的内容效度，选出最佳量表后对244个来自49个模型家族的LLMs进行施测。通过方差分析检验模型间差异，通过因子分析检验结构效度，并通过比较基础模型与指令微调模型分析对齐训练的影响。

Result: 1) 改编版项目可达足够内容效度，原始项目不行；2) 模型间变异仅占总分方差3%，无法捕获有意义差异；3) LLM响应未恢复大五因子结构，开放性、尽责性、外向性、宜人性四个维度高度相关（r≥0.92）；4) 对齐训练系统性地将分数向社会期望方向偏移。

Conclusion: 大五人格量表在LLMs中测量的不是与人类人格等价的构念，直接套用会为LLM的基准测试、系统比较和治理决策提供误导性结论。应开发专为LLMs设计的评估框架，而非未经验证地借用人类构念。

Abstract: Human personality inventories are increasingly used to characterize large language models (LLMs), compare systems, and inform downstream governance claims. Yet, these inventories were developed and validated for humans, and it remains unclear whether they apply to LLMs. We present a systematic psychometric evaluation of Big Five personality measurements in LLMs. We ask three research questions: Do Big Five inventories a) appropriately describe LLMs, b) capture inter-individual differences across models, and c) reflect internal factors consistent with human personality. We assess content validity of five candidate Big Five inventories and administer the winning inventory to N = 244 different models spanning 49 model families. First, we found that Big Five items adapted for LLMs can reach sufficient content validity, while original human-developed items did not. Second, Big Five inventories did not capture meaningful differences between LLMs: We found low variability between models, accounting for only 3% of total score variance. Third, LLMs responses did not recover the Big Five five-factor structure with four of the Big Five facets collapsing into one (r >= .92). Direct comparisons between base and instruction-tuned model variants suggested that alignment training systematically shifted Big Five scores toward socially desirable traits. These findings demonstrate that Big Five scores do not measure a construct equivalent to human personality in LLMs. Applying human personality frameworks to LLMs produces misleading characterizations used to benchmark, compare, and govern LLMs. We highlight the need for evaluation frameworks that are developed for LLMs, rather than adopting human constructs without validation.

</details>


### [116] [Physical surfaces make touch interactions in virtual reality precise, efficient, and bimanual](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02430)
*Wen Ying,Seongkook Heo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究对比了VR中三种触觉反馈（无反馈、触觉反馈、实体表面）对高精度操作任务的影响，发现便携实体表面在选取精度、追踪效率和草图质量上表现最佳，且用户偏好更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR系统中的空中手势缺乏触觉反馈和物理参考面，导致精确触控交互困难，本文旨在探究不同手部接地触觉反馈对高精度VR任务的影响。

Method: 设计了三种触觉反馈条件（无反馈、触觉反馈、实体表面），通过选取、追踪和草图绘制三类高精度任务评估用户表现和偏好。

Result: 便携实体表面在选取精度、追踪效率和草图质量上最优，且参与者在实体表面任务中表现出更高的双手协同使用率和更强的控制感与自信心。

Conclusion: 实体物理表面作为触觉参照能显著提升VR高精度交互的性能和用户体验，其带来的自信感和控制感是用户偏好的关键因素。

Abstract: Virtual reality (VR) systems can enable convenient hand-based interactions across diverse work scenarios. However, mid-air gestures lack tactile feedback and a physical reference surface to support the hand. This absence of haptic grounding can cause significant challenges in achieving precise and efficient touch interactions. This paper investigates the effect of different types of hand-grounded haptic feedback on the touch performance of VR tasks that demand high precision, such as selecting, tracing, and sketching. We compared three levels of haptic feedback: 1) No Haptic Feedback, where only visual feedback was provided; 2) Tactile Feedback, where users received vibrotactile and pressure feedback upon touching a virtual surface; 3) Physical Surface, where users interacted with a portable and tangible surface.
  Our study found that portable physical surfaces enabled the best selection precision, tracing efficiency, and sketch quality. Furthermore, participants showed increased bimanual hand utilization when engaging with a physical surface during tasks. These observed behaviors corresponded to participants' preference for interacting with physical surfaces, attributed to a better sense of confidence and control.

</details>


### [117] [When Do LLM Personas Support Visualization Design? A Cross-Model Study of Color Assignment and Chart Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02455)
*Shahreen Salim,Klaus Mueller*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了大语言模型人格角色在可视化设计中的稳定性，发现人格-颜色关联高度依赖模型配置，而图表选择中任务上下文比人格特质更能主导偏好，表明LLM人格角色仅适合作为探索性工具而非人类参与者的替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM人格角色被越来越多地用于近似不同用户群体以辅助早期可视化设计，但人格条件化的输出究竟是反映稳定的人格效应，还是模型选择和任务框架的人为产物，这一问题尚不明确。

Method: 在颜色分配（抽象/具体概念）和图表偏好评分两项可视化任务中，使用43个大五人格剖面，在GPT-4o-mini、GPT-4.1-mini和GPT-5-mini三个模型上进行系统比较分析。

Result: 人格-颜色耦合高度依赖模型配置：GPT-4o-mini全无此效应，GPT-4.1-mini全有，GPT-5-mini仅部分概念存在。抽象概念中人格解释的色相方差大于模型身份，而具体概念中两者效应相当且更小。图表选择中，无人格基线在9个模型-上下文组合中的8个恢复了相同的top-1选择。

Conclusion: LLM人格角色仅适合作为可视化设计的探索性探针，不能替代真实人类被试，未来研究应采用多模型测试、概念类型拆分和无人格基线对照。

Abstract: Large language model personas are increasingly used to approximate diverse users during early-stage visualization design, but it remains unclear whether persona-conditioned outputs reflect stable personality effects or artifacts of model choice and task framing. We examine this question across two visualization-relevant tasks: color assignment for abstract and concrete concepts, and chart-idiom preference ratings across task contexts. Using 43 Big Five profiles across GPT-4o-mini, GPT-4.1-mini, and GPT-5-mini, we find that personality-color coupling is highly model-configuration dependent: absent in GPT-4o-mini for all six concepts, consistent in GPT-4.1-mini across all six, and partial in GPT-5-mini for two of six. Concept type further shapes the signal: for abstract concepts, personality explains more hue variance than model identity, while concrete concepts show smaller and comparable effects. In chart choice, trait-aligned cluster aggregation produces stable top-idiom rankings across all nine cluster-context combinations, but a no-persona baseline recovers the same top choice in 8 of 9 model-context cells, indicating that task context drives rank-1 selection more than personality. These findings position LLM personas as exploratory probes for visualization design, not substitutes for human participants, and motivate multi-model testing, concept-type disaggregation, and no-persona baselines in future studies.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Folding an e-graph in pure egglog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01249)
*Zeng Ren,Maddy Bowers*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了在纯egglog中对递归数据结构进行折叠（catamorphism）的方法，利用e-graph的e-class实现自动记忆化和减少计算量，并讨论了分阶段推理规则以避免值重新传播的实现技巧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 折叠（catamorphism）是函数式编程中递归计算声明式定义的核心操作，将其应用于e-graph可以获得自动记忆化和计算量大幅减少（子问题定义在e-class而非具体子项上）的好处。

Method: 在纯egglog中定义并实现了通用的折叠（catamorphism）概念，采用了一种可复用的实现技巧——分阶段组织推理规则，以避免更新值的重新传播。

Result: 实现了在e-graph上下文中有效的折叠操作，通过e-class定义子问题减少了计算量，并解决了推理规则中值重新传播的问题。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在egglog中实现catamorphism的可行性，并为后续e-graph上的递归计算提供了基础性工具和实现模式。

Abstract: Folding over a recursive data structure (also known as "catamorphism") is an essential operation in functional programming. It allows one to declaratively define a recursive computation by focusing on how solutions of subproblems are combined into the solution of the parent problem. Folding in the context of e-graphs can have several benefits. Not only do we get memoization for free, but also computation is drastically reduced since the subproblems are defined over e-classes as opposed to concrete sub-terms. This short report presents our initial attempt at defining and implementing catamorphism, a general notion of a fold, in pure egglog. We discuss a reusable implementation trick to stage the inference rules so that there is no re-propagation of updated values.

</details>


### [119] [Trustworthy Runtime Verification via Bisimulation (Extended Experience Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01363)
*Ryan G. Scott,Ivan Perez,Alwyn E. Goodloe,Mike Dodds,Robert Dockins*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出CopilotVerifier，它伴随Copilot编译器运行，为编译输出生成正确性证明，确保监控程序与其编译形式在等价输入下产生等价输出，且两者在相同情况下崩溃或不崩溃，通过构建于Crucible和What4工具之上，以适中成本显著提升编译器可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键系统中使用运行时验证时，监控代码必须行为正确。Copilot框架从Haskell嵌入的DSL自动生成C监控程序，但审计人员难以确信编译后的监控程序不会崩溃且实现了Copilot语义所需的行为，因此需要形式化保证。

Method: CopilotVerifier伴随Copilot编译器运行，生成正确性证明。证明形式化为一个双模拟关系（bisimulation），分解为一组验证条件。使用基于SMT的两项技术：Crucible符号执行库（用于LLVM）和What4求解器接口库。

Result: 结果表明，通过构建于现有工具之上，可以以适中成本大幅提升编译器可信度，为最终生成令人类审计人员信服的形式化保证论证铺平了道路。

Conclusion: CopilotVerifier能够验证编译输出的正确性，在安全关键领域具有重要价值，未来目标是生成令人类审计人员信服的形式化保证论证。

Abstract: When runtime verification is used to monitor safety-critical systems, it is essential that monitoring code behaves correctly. The Copilot runtime verification framework pursues this goal by automatically generating C monitor programs from a high-level DSL embedded in Haskell. In safety-critical domains, every piece of deployed code must be accompanied by an assurance argument that is convincing to human auditors. However, it is difficult for auditors to determine with confidence that a compiled monitor cannot crash and implements the behavior required by the Copilot semantics.
  In this paper we describe CopilotVerifier, which runs alongside the Copilot compiler, generating a proof of correctness for the compiled output. The proof establishes that a given Copilot monitor and its compiled form produce equivalent outputs on equivalent inputs, and that they either crash in identical circumstances or cannot crash. The proof takes the form of a bisimulation broken down into a set of verification conditions. We leverage two pieces of SMT-backed technology: the Crucible symbolic execution library for LLVM and the What4 solver interface library. Our results demonstrate that dramatically increased compiler assurance can be achieved at moderate cost by building on existing tools. This paves the way to our ultimate goal of generating formal assurance arguments that are convincing to human auditors.

</details>


<div id='cs.SC'></div>

# cs.SC [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [When Algebraic Symmetry Breaking Meets Solvers: An Experimental Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01726)
*Madalina Erascu,Johannes Middeke*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 本文实验评估了整数线性规划中自动生成的多项式对称性破缺约束，比较了不同求解器处理方式（二次处理、内部重构、显式线性化）在近半容量装箱基准问题上的效果。结果表明，多项式对称性破缺的有效性高度依赖求解器，紧凑的二次破缺族可提升性能，而线性化、大型破缺集或求解器重构可能因模型增大或搜索行为恶化而抵消收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估自动生成的多项式对称性破缺约束在整数线性规划中的实际效果，特别是验证其是否能够通用地提升求解器性能。

Method: 基于ISSAC 2026提出的方法，在近半容量装箱基准问题上，比较求解器原生二次处理、求解器内部重构和显式线性化三种方式，使用多个数学规划求解器和SMT求解器进行实验。

Result: 多项式对称性破缺的效果高度依赖求解器；紧凑二次破缺族可提升性能；线性化、大型破缺集和求解器重构可能因模型增大或搜索行为恶化而抵消性能收益。

Conclusion: 自动生成的对称性破缺约束应以求解器感知的方式评估，而非视为与求解器无关的模型附加项。

Abstract: We present an experimental evaluation of automatically generated polynomial symmetry breaking constraints for integer linear programs. Starting from the method that we introduced at the International Symposium on Symbolic and Algebraic Computation (ISSAC) 2026, we compare solver native quadratic handling, solver-internal reformulation, and explicit linearization on near half-capacity bin-packing benchmarks.
  Experiments with several mathematical programming solvers and satisfiability modulo theory solvers show that the effectiveness of polynomial symmetry breaking is strongly solver-dependent. Compact quadratic breaker families can improve performance, whereas linearization, large breaker sets, or solver reformulations may offset these gains through increased model size or less favorable search behavior. These results suggest that automatically generated symmetry breakers should be evaluated in a solver-aware manner rather than treated as solver-independent additions to a model.

</details>


### [121] [Local polynomial factorisation: improving the Montes algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02153)
*Poteaux Adrien,Weimann Martin*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 本文显著改进了Nart-Montes算法，通过推广广义Newton多边形下的Hensel引理并采用分治策略，实现了在完备离散赋值环上多项式分解的几乎最优复杂度，将复杂度降低了判别式赋值δ倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Nart-Montes算法在完备离散赋值环上多项式分解的效率，降低计算复杂度。

Method: 1) 在广义Newton多边形上下文中推广Hensel引理；2) 提出新的分治策略；3) 证明在剩余特征为零或足够大时，近似根可作为类型的便捷代表。

Result: 实现了几乎最优的复杂度，对于不可约性判定和分解问题，复杂度降至剩余域上方分解成本加上判别式赋值δ倍的改进。

Conclusion: 该方法将OM-分解的计算复杂度降低了因子δ（判别式赋值），达到了近乎最优的效率。

Abstract: We improve significantly the Nart-Montes algorithm for factoring
  polynomials over a complete discrete valuation ring $\mathbb{A}$. Our first
  contribution is to extend the Hensel lemma in the context of
  generalised Newton polygons, from which we derive a new divide and
  conquer strategy. Also, if $\mathbb{A}$ has residual characteristic zero or
  high enough, we prove that approximate roots are convenient
  representatives of types, leading finally to an almost optimal
  complexity both for irreducibility and factorisation issues, plus
  the cost of factorisations above the residue field. For instance, to
  compute an OM-factorisation of $F\in\mathbb{A}[x]$, we improve the
  complexity by a factor $δ$, the
  discriminant valuation of $F$.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [CADENZA in Action: Breaking the Monolith with Intent-Dependent Plan Spaces for Semantic Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01468)
*Jaehyun Ha,Yongjoo Park,Wook-Shin Han*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: CADENZA是一个语义查询优化器，将自然语言意图分解为多个步骤，为每个步骤选择具体的物理实现并调优参数，在用户指定的质量-延迟-成本偏好下进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器以单体实现（如LLM、嵌入模型）的粒度优化语义算子，导致昂贵的模型调用与无法捕获意图依赖语义的廉价替代方案之间存在权衡。

Method: 将意图编译为分解步骤，为每个步骤选择具体物理实现，并在用户指定的质量-延迟-成本偏好下调优参数。

Result: 通过Web界面演示，展示意图如何分解为替代计划、每个计划如何优化以及不同偏好如何产生不同优胜计划。

Conclusion: CADENZA通过细粒度的步骤分解和物理实现选择，打破了传统语义算子的优化权衡，实现了更灵活的质量-延迟-成本折中。

Abstract: Semantic query processing engines execute semantic operators, whose behavior is specified by natural-language intents, via model inference over multimodal data. Most existing optimizers optimize the operators at the granularity of monolithic implementations -- such as LLMs and embedding models -- forcing a trade-off between expensive model calls and cheaper alternatives that fail to capture intent-dependent semantics. We present CADENZA, a semantic operator optimizer that compiles an intent into decomposed steps, selects concrete physical implementations for each step, and tunes their parameters under user-specified quality-latency-cost preferences. In this demonstration, users interact with CADENZA through a web interface over multimodal databases, exploring how an intent is decomposed into alternative plans, how each plan is optimized, and how different preferences yield different winning plans.

</details>


### [123] [The General Stability of Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01546)
*Houming Chen,H. V. Jagadish*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于距离的通用稳定性（general stability）概念，作为传统精确匹配稳定性的推广，允许用户根据实际应用定义哪些排序变化是重要的，并设计了相应的计算算法（二维扫描算法、无偏多维采样器以及针对拟凸距离函数的Conv-SC方法），实验表明该方法在真实数据集上有效且可扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于精确匹配的体积稳定性过于粗糙：一旦权重扰动导致排序发生变化，无论这种变化是颠覆了首位还是仅仅交换了两个排名靠后的近邻项，都被一视同仁地视为不稳定。这种"一刀切"的方式无法区分不同排序变化的重要性，需要一种能根据应用场景区分排序变化严重程度的稳定性度量。

Method: 本文提出通用稳定性（general stability），通过用户定义的距离函数聚合排序区域，将精确稳定性作为特例纳入。算法方面：(1) 二维扫描算法；(2) 无偏多维采样器；(3) 针对拟凸距离函数的Conv-SC方法，将稳定性计算转化为凸体积逼近问题，可利用随机多项式时间算法求解。

Result: 在8个真实数据集和生成实例上的实验表明：(1) 距离敏感的稳定性在真实数据上能提供有意义的结果；(2) 所提估计器准确且实用；(3) Conv-SC方法在高维场景下对拟凸距离函数具有更好的扩展性。

Conclusion: 通用稳定性为排序稳定性分析提供了更灵活的框架，使研究者能够根据具体应用需求定义排序变化的严重程度。拟凸距离函数作为可处理子类的发现，为大规模高维场景下的稳定性计算提供了可行的近似方案。

Abstract: Rankings derived from weighted scoring functions are widely used in settings such as university rankings and employment candidate evaluations. Since ranking weights are often chosen by organizations or analysts, ranking stability asks whether a reported ranking persists under reasonable weight changes. Prior work on stable rankings formalizes this idea through volume-based stability, which measures the fraction of the weight space that induces the target ranking exactly.
  This exact-match requirement can be too blunt: once a perturbed weight vector produces a different ranking, exact stability gives it no credit, whether the change replaces the top-ranked item or only swaps two nearly tied lower-ranked items. We propose general stability, a distance-based generalization that aggregates ranking regions according to a user-defined distance from the target ranking. This lets users specify which ranking changes matter in the application, while recovering exact stability as a special case.
  Our algorithmic focus is stability computation: given a reported or user-specified ranking and a distance function, estimate its general-stability score. We give a two-dimensional sweep algorithm and an unbiased multidimensional sampler that extend exact-stability methods, and analyze why sampling can scale poorly as the dimension grows. Motivated by this scaling challenge, we identify quasiconvex distance functions as a tractable subclass and introduce Conv-SC, which reduces stability computation for this subclass to convex-volume approximation, where randomized polynomial-time methods are available. Experiments on eight real datasets and generated instances show that distance-sensitive stability gives informative real-data results, that our estimators are accurate and practical, and that Conv-SC improves scaling with dimension for quasiconvex distance functions.

</details>


### [124] [pykci: A Compact Urban Knowledge Graph for Semantic and Spatial Queries using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01605)
*Huynh Duc An Son Nguyen,Lukas Arzoumanidis,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: pykci 是一个开源系统，能将 CityGML 2.0 数据集转换为 Neo4j 知识图谱，并通过大语言模型实现自然语言查询，无需用户掌握专业查询语言和数据库模式知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CityGML 虽然包含丰富的语义、几何和拓扑信息，但其 XML 编码设计用于数据交换而非分析，关系型映射又需要专业知识，导致非专家用户难以直接查询和利用这些语义 3D 城市模型数据。

Method: 构建了一个覆盖 CityGML 2.0 所有主题要素模块和细节层次的知识图谱，采用 R-tree 空间索引，通过端到端 Python 管道完成数据导入、3D Tiles 可视化导出和回写。查询端使用模型无关的 text-to-Cypher 机制，将图谱模式作为上下文供给大语言模型，将自然语言问题翻译为 Cypher 查询执行。

Result: 在德国汉堡的开放政府 CityGML LoD2 数据集上进行了验证，支持复杂语义和空间查询（如识别适合绿化的屋顶表面）。支持本地开源模型（保护敏感城市数据上）和商业模型两种方案，回答基于真实城市数据而非模型参数记忆，减少了幻觉并提供了可审计的溯源。

Conclusion: pykci 使城市规划师、GIS 从业者和市民无需掌握查询语言和数据库模式知识即可与语义 3D 城市模型进行交互，降低了 CityGML 数据的使用门槛。

Abstract: CityGML, the OGC standard for modeling, storage, and exchange of semantic 3D city models, describes urban objects with detailed semantics, geometry, and topology. Yet this richness is difficult to query directly: CityGML's XML encoding is designed for exchange rather than analysis, and relational mappings expose it through schemas requiring expert knowledge. We present pykci (Python Knowledge Graph for Cities), an open-source system that transforms CityGML 2.0 datasets into a compact urban knowledge graph in Neo4j and makes it queryable in natural language. The graph schema covers all thematic feature modules of CityGML 2.0 across all levels of detail and is spatially indexed with an R-tree for efficient geometric retrieval. A complete end-to-end Python pipeline ingests CityGML datasets into the knowledge graph, exports them to OGC 3D Tiles for interactive visualization, and supports lossless round-trip export of all content back to CityGML. For querying, the graph is paired with a large language model through a model-agnostic text-to-Cypher mechanism: the graph schema is supplied as context, and the model translates natural-language questions into Cypher queries executed against the graph. We evaluate both a locally running open-weight model, which keeps sensitive city data on-premise, and a state-of-the-art commercial model for the most demanding spatial and semantic queries. Answers are grounded in exact city data rather than the model's parametric memory, reducing hallucination and providing auditable provenance for every response. We demonstrate the system on open-government CityGML LoD2 datasets from Hamburg, Germany, including complex semantic and spatial queries such as identifying roof surfaces suitable for greening. pykci enables urban planners, GIS practitioners, and citizens to interact with semantic 3D city models without expertise in query languages and database schemas.

</details>


### [125] [AgenticDataBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01647)
*Zhaoyan Sun,Shan Zhong,Daizhou Wen,Jiaxing Han,Guoliang Li,Ying Yan,Peng Zhang,Yu Su,Xiang Qi,Baolin Sun,Chengyuan Yang,Tao Fang,Huaiyu Ruan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出了AgenticDataBench基准测试，用于评估基于大语言模型的数据智能体在数据科学工作流自动化中的表现，涵盖15个垂直领域（含5个真实金融B2B用例），通过数据科学技能量化覆盖度，并提供了细粒度的技能级评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据智能体缺乏全面、细粒度的基准来评估其在多样化数据科学场景中的表现。

Method: 1) 收集15个垂直领域的真实数据集与任务（含5个B2B金融用例）；2) 引入数据科学技能概念，通过Stack Overflow上聚类提取代表性技能；3) 选择技能组合多样性最大的任务-解决方案对；4) 针对缺乏真实数据的领域，提出基于LLM的系统化任务生成方法；5) 在开源测试平台上评估前沿数据智能体。

Result: 构建了覆盖多样化领域和技能组合的基准，提供了对现有数据智能体在技能级别的详细性能分析。

Conclusion: AgenticDataBench填补了数据科学自动化评估领域的空白，为后续数据智能体的研究提供了标准化评测框架和细粒度性能洞察。

Abstract: Data science aims to derive actionable insights from heterogeneous raw data, unlocking the value of the massive amounts of data generated in modern society. Automating this process is essential to reducing labor-intensive efforts for data scientists and enabling scalable data-driven applications. Recently, large language model (LLM)-based data agents have emerged as a promising solution to automate data science workflows. However, the field lacks comprehensive benchmarks to rigorously evaluate these agents across diverse scenarios with fine-grained granularity. To address this gap, we propose AgenticDataBench, a comprehensive benchmark featuring realistic tasks spanning diverse domains with fine-grained ground-truth labels. This enables evaluations to capture the diversity and complexity of data science workflows and the detailed performance of agents. First, to cover diverse domains, we collect real datasets and tasks from 15 vertical domains, including 5 real-world B2B use cases from a leading fintech company. Second, to remove redundancy in real-world tasks and generate high-quality tasks for domains lacking real data, we introduce data science skills, recurring data-centric operational patterns, and quantify benchmark coverage by the number of skills included. Representative skills are extracted from large-scale task solutions on Stack Overflow using skill-aligned hierarchical clustering. Third, for real-world business tasks, we select task-solution pairs that maximize diversity in skill composition, ensuring broad coverage of practical scenarios. Fourth, to generate realistic tasks for devise domains without real tasks, we propose a systematic LLM-based task generation approach to create workflows and tasks based on these skills. Finally, we evaluate state-of-the-art data agents using our annotated benchmark and open-sourced testbed, providing detailed skill-level insights.

</details>


### [126] [HNSW with Accuracy Guarantees Using Graph Spanners -- A Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02338)
*Minghao Li,Raghav Mittal,Sanjivni Rana,Suraj Shetiya,Gautam Das,Nick Koudas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一种"Certify-then-Rectify"框架，通过统计认证器动态评估HNSW搜索质量，在低质量时切换到精确恢复算法，利用极值理论估计几何伸展因子以保障最坏情况下的正确性，同时保持平均情况下的HNSW速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HNSW图在业界广泛使用，但其贪心图遍历启发式算法缺乏正确性的理论保证，现有方法无法同时兼顾启发式搜索的速度和精确检索的严谨性。

Method: 提出"Certify-then-Rectify"两阶段框架：1) 使用无分布统计认证器以最小开销动态评估HNSW搜索结果质量；2) 当认证表明结果质量低时，安全升级到精确恢复算法；3) 将HNSW图重新解释为几何斯潘纳图，利用极值理论随机估计其最大经验伸展因子，从而数学上界定真实最近邻的最大距离。

Result: 在基准数据集上的广泛评估表明，该分层框架在平均情况下保持HNSW的速度，同时确保最坏情况下的精确搜索正确性，性能优于其他适用方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种兼顾HNSW速度与精确检索保证的新框架，通过统计认证与极值理论相结合，弥合了启发式搜索与精确检索之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Hierarchical Navigable Small World (HNSW) graphs serve as the industry standard due to their logarithmic complexity and strong empirical performance. However, HNSW relies on greedy graph traversal, a heuristic that provides no theoretical guarantees of correctness. In this paper, we propose a novel "Certify-then-Rectify" framework that bridges the gap between the speed of heuristic search and the rigor of exact retrieval. Rather than discarding HNSW, our approach first employs a distribution-free statistical certifier to dynamically evaluate the quality of a standard HNSW search with minimal overhead. If certification indicates that the retrieved neighbors are of low quality, the framework safely escalates to a rigorous exact recovery algorithm. To make this exact recovery computationally feasible, we reinterpret the HNSW graph as a geometric spanner and utilize Extreme Value Theory to stochastically estimate its maximum empirical stretch factor. This allows us to mathematically bound the maximum distance of true nearest neighbors. Extensive evaluations on benchmark datasets demonstrate that our tiered framework delivers the average-case speed of HNSW while ensuring the worst-case correctness of exact search and outperforming other applicable approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多层Q矩阵嵌入神经网络（M-QCDNet），将认知诊断模型的解释性与深度学习相结合，通过Q矩阵约束技能-项目关系，并设计带L2惩罚的损失函数平衡预测性能与结构对齐，同时开发了可解释对齐度量指标，实现可解释、公平且可操作的认知诊断AI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知诊断模型（CDMs）具有良好的结构可解释性但表达能力有限，而深度神经网络（NN）预测能力强但缺乏可解释性，两者难以兼顾。研究者希望桥接心理测量透明性与神经网络的灵活性，构建兼具解释性和预测性能的认知诊断模型。

Method: 采用多层神经网络架构，将Q矩阵作为结构先验嵌入网络以建模项目-技能关系；设计带L2惩罚项的损失函数，对与Q矩阵不一致的技能激活进行惩罚；开发基于对齐的可解释性度量指标，量化预测技能激活与项目级技能之间的对应程度。

Result: 提出了可解释对齐度量指标，能有效量化预测技能激活与项目技能的对齐程度；模型在保持预测性能的同时，确保了潜在掌握图谱的可解释性及其与认知理论的一致性。

Conclusion: M-QCDNet成功整合了心理测量透明性与神经网络的灵活性，能早期检测学习困难并支持基于掌握程度的干预，为课堂实践提供了实用价值，推动认知诊断AI向可解释、公平和可操作方向发展。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [128] [I\textsuperscript{2}RiMA: Spectral Riemannian Representation with Temporal Attention for Mental Stress Detection based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01279)
*Cheng He,Kunyu Peng,Shangen Han,Jinming Ma,Jinhong Ding,Likun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network，通过频域SPD切空间映射、频率簇聚合和跨切片注意力机制，解决跨被试EEG压力检测中个体差异和频域特异性问题，在三个数据集上以1.60M参数和31.95M FLOPs达到82.78%平衡准确率，优于五个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黎曼方法主要在时域建模空间协方差，忽略了对高级认知状态解码至关重要的神经振荡信息；标准时间tokenization会破坏切片间的时间连贯性。跨被试EEG压力检测中，判别性压力相关模式既依赖于被试个体又具有频率特异性。

Method: (1) 在每个频点独立构建空间协方差矩阵并映射到SPD切空间，保留通道间几何结构和频率特异性判别信息；(2) 频率簇聚合机制选择信息丰富的频谱成分，形成与EEG节律对齐的紧凑数据驱动频率簇以减少冗余；(3) 切片内-切片间注意力模块自适应融合局部切片级频谱动态和全局时间上下文。

Result: 在三个数据集上一致优于五个SOTA基线，最高平衡准确率82.78%，参数量仅1.60M，FLOPs为31.95M。

Conclusion: 该方法通过频域黎曼几何建模和双尺度注意力机制，有效解决了跨被试EEG压力检测中的频域特异性和个体差异问题，兼具高精度和高效率。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG stress detection remains challenging because discriminative stress-related patterns are both subject-dependent and frequency-specific. Conventional Riemannian methods model spatial covariance mainly in the time domain, overlooking neural oscillations that are critical for high-level cognitive state decoding, while standard temporal tokenization often fragments inter-slice temporal coherence. To address these limitations, we propose \method{}, an Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network for EEG-based stress detection. \method{} constructs spatial covariance matrices independently at each frequency point and maps them to the SPD tangent space, preserving channel-wise geometry together with frequency-specific discriminative cues. It further introduces frequency cluster aggregation to select informative spectral components and reduce redundancy by forming compact, data-driven frequency clusters aligned with EEG rhythms. Finally, an intra-inter slice attention module adaptively integrates local slice-level spectral dynamics and global temporal context across EEG sequences. Experiments on three datasets show that \method{} consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 82.78\% balanced accuracy while remaining efficient with only 1.60M parameters and 31.95M FLOPs.

</details>


### [129] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究编程示例（PBE）系统的对抗性鲁棒性，提出了一种针对有限版本空间的固定集最坏情况破坏形式化方法，并引入版本空间分区聚合（VPA）防御机制。核心发现是：低裕度PBE任务存在对抗性鲁棒性维度，而传统的随机噪声评估无法捕捉这一维度；VPA仅在干净语义能保持分区投票裕度时有效，这在现实任务中常不成立。实验在多个基准上验证了这一边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PBE系统通常假设示例错误来自随机噪声过程，但忽略了对抗性攻击场景——攻击者知晓合成器后选择性地破坏示例，使得返回的程序受到最大损害。本文旨在形式化并评估这种对抗性破坏对PBE系统的影响。

Method: 1) 形式化有限PBE版本空间的固定集最坏情况破坏；2) 对字符串变换DSL实现精确（有界池内）和启发式破坏搜索；3) 提出版本空间分区聚合（VPA）防御：在不相交示例组上分别合成，通过语义签名投票聚合结果。

Result: 1) 单个精心设计的编辑即可使全部8个spike任务失败，而200次随机噪声对照的成功率仅10.3%-16.7%；2) 生成的低裕度（margin-1）任务在预算1下被破坏，VPA可恢复；3) 公共SyGuS任务投票裕度接近1，自适应攻击使VPA精度归零；4) Playgol上VPA对141个接受行显示正向配对bootstrap优势；5) 20个控制margin-1任务的LLM实验呈现相同的clean-to-attacked模式。

Conclusion: 低裕度PBE任务存在对抗性鲁棒性维度，传统随机噪声评估无法捕获；VPA防御仅在干净语义能保持分区投票裕度时有效，而这在现实任务中常常不成立。论文的论断是审慎且部分负面的：对抗性鲁棒性是PBE的一个真实维度，但简单的投票防御有根本性局限。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [130] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于领域知识的图卷积网络（GCN）用于心电图识别，通过双流有向图建模心电周期内和周期间的空间与时间依赖关系，在首届中国心电图智能竞赛数据集上取得了88.1%的平均F1分数，对罕见类别的F1分数达76.3%，优于现有最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管人工智能在医疗领域广泛应用，但AI模型的可解释性仍然不足，尤其是在心电图识别等专业领域中。现有的端到端卷积神经网络缺乏对心电图领域知识的显式利用。

Method: 提出基于领域知识的图卷积网络，将PRQST关键点作为领域知识融入模型。采用双流有向图结构：空间有向图捕捉关键点间的位置关系，时间有向图描述相邻心电周期间的时序依赖关系。

Result: 在首届中国心电图智能竞赛数据集上，模型总体平均F1分数为88.1%，罕见类别平均F1分数为76.3%，均优于现有最优模型。

Conclusion: 引入领域知识能够有效提升心电图识别的检测性能，尤其对罕见类别的识别有显著改善。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles. Speci cally, spatial directed graphs capture the positional relationships among key points, while temporal directed graphs delineate temporal dependencies between adjacent cycles in extended ECG sequences. Experimental re sults on the First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition dataset, which speci cally classify ECG into nine categories, prove the e cacy of the proposed model. The overall average F1 score is 88.1%, the average F1 score of rare categories is 76.3%, both outperform the state-of-the-art models. The introduction of domain knowledge did enhance the detec tion performance, especially for rare categories.

</details>


### [131] [Wind-Aware Reinforcement Learning Control of a Small Quadrotor Using Learned Onboard Wind Estimation in Simulated Atmospheric Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01528)
*Abdullah Al Tasim,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段学习框架：先用注意力增强的GRU网络从机载数据估计局部风速（RMSE 0.40 m/s，方向误差3.2°），再将冻结的估计器输出输入给PPO强化学习控制器，使水平轨迹跟踪误差相对于风盲PD基线降低48%，在4-12 m/s平均风速下100%胜出，并能在基线失效的13-15 m/s极端风中优雅退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型多旋翼飞行器在受湍流风影响的大气边界层中执行任务时，风扰动会严重降低轨迹跟踪精度，甚至使传统反馈控制失效，因此需要有效的风估计与抗风控制方法。

Method: 两阶段学习流水线：(1) 风估计器——注意力增强的门控循环网络（GRU），在数千次含冯·卡门湍流、幂律风切变和偏转的模拟飞行数据上训练；(2) PPO强化学习控制器，接收冻结的风估计器输出作为状态输入，通过策略优化实现抗风控制。并通过三路消融实验分离运动学分量和风感知分量的贡献。

Result: 风估计器在未见风况下水平风速RMSE 0.40 m/s、方向误差3.2°，精度接近未解析湍流造成的下限；对垂直爬升廓线的技能评分0.861。PPO控制器在4-12 m/s平均风速下轨迹跟踪误差降低48%，100%评估回合优于PD基线。消融实验显示风感知贡献随风速增大，从低风时很小到强风时约占总体改善的一半。

Conclusion: 将数据驱动的风估计与RL控制结合，能显著提升多旋翼在湍流环境中的轨迹跟踪性能，且对超出训练分布的极端风况具有优雅退化能力，验证了该框架的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Small multirotor aircraft are increasingly tasked with operations in the atmospheric boundary layer, where turbulent winds comparable to the vehicle's airspeed degrade trajectory tracking and can defeat conventional feedback control. This work illustrates a two-stage learning pipeline that first estimates the local wind from onboard kinematics and dynamics and then exploits that estimate inside a reinforcement learning (RL) flight controller. The wind estimator, an attention-augmented gated recurrent network trained on thousands of simulated flights through von Karman turbulence with power-law shear and veer, recovers the horizontal wind vector with a per-flight root-mean-square error of 0.40 m/s and a direction error of 3.2 degrees on unseen wind regimes, an accuracy near the floor imposed by unresolved turbulence, and generalizes to vertical ascent profiles with a skill score of 0.861 over a constant-wind reference. A proximal policy optimization controller receiving the frozen estimator's output reduces horizontal trajectory tracking error by 48% relative to a wind-blind proportional-derivative baseline across mean winds of 4 m/s to 12 m/s, winning on 100% of evaluation episodes. A three-way ablation decomposes this improvement into a kinematic component, available without wind information, and a wind-perception component; the perception share rises with wind speed, from small in light winds toward roughly half the total benefit in strong winds, consistent with the quadratic scaling of aerodynamic drag. The controller degrades gracefully on out-of-distribution winds of 13 m/s to 15 m/s, where the baseline fails catastrophically.

</details>


### [132] [Scaling Laws for Grid-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01283)
*Matthew J Liu,Wei Hang Zheng,Vidhan Purohit,Siqi Xie,Chieh-En Li,Jerry Li,Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多探针网格算法在ANN搜索中的表现，发现在GloVe嵌入族上存在维度缩放交叉现象：网格算法保持近似恒定的维度缩放指数，而图、树和分区方法的吞吐量下降。该方法具有近线性查询扩展和较低索引成本，在重建密集或高维场景下具有竞争力，并对高效Transformer架构的成本分析有指导意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格方法在现代ANN缩放分析中缺失，本文旨在系统表征多探针网格算法在数据集大小N和维度d上的性能特征。

Method: 对多探针网格算法进行系统实验，在GloVe嵌入族上对比图、树和分区方法，分析N和d的缩放性质。

Result: 发现维度缩放交叉现象：网格算法保持近似恒定的维度缩放指数，其他方法吞吐量下降；网格算法具有近线性查询扩展和更低索引成本。

Conclusion: 网格方法在重建密集或高维场景下具有竞争力；ANN算法的缩放性质可指导高效Transformer架构的成本分析。

Abstract: Grid-based approaches to approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search have been absent from modern scaling analyses. We present a systematic characterization of a multiprobe grid algorithm with respect to dataset size $N$ and dimensionality $d$. Our experiments reveal a previously unreported $d$-scaling crossover on the GloVe embedding family, in which multiprobe grid search maintains an approximately constant dimensional scaling exponent while other graph-, tree-, and partitioning-based methods exhibit degrading throughput. The advantage comes with near-linear query scaling in $N$, but also with lower indexing cost than competing ANN methods. Our results suggest that grid-based methods such as multiprobe grid may be competitive in rebuild-heavy or high-dimensional settings where indexing cost and dimensional robustness dictate performance. More broadly, recent work has formalized self-attention as an ANN operation. Thus, the $N$- and $d$-scaling properties of ANN algorithms may guide cost analysis of efficient transformer architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/weiz345/MultiProbeANN.

</details>


### [133] [Predicting Closed-Loop Performance of Latent World Models: Offline Checkpoint Selection for MPC and Model-Based RL Under Non-Markovian Rewards in LunarLander](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01736)
*Nikolai Smolyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何仅通过验证时的诊断指标来预测潜在世界模型的下游闭环性能。针对LunarLander v3环境，提出了一套基于最优控制理论的结构化验证诊断指标，发现奖励可观测性分数(ROF)是闭环性能的最佳单一预测因子，并将其与三个结构正则化器组合为复合奖励可观测性分数(CROF)。CROF选出的世界模型训练的基于模型的A2C策略在减少约65倍真实环境交互的同时，比无模型A2C基线高出约24.5回报点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型训练中，验证损失和多步预测RMSE在闭环性能崩溃后仍持续改善，导致难以通过传统验证指标选择最佳检查点，因此需要更有效的离线诊断指标来预测下游闭环性能。

Method: 从最优控制理论中提取一套结构化验证诊断指标，在LunarLander v3环境中训练RSSM世界模型，以CEM-MPC回报作为闭环性能的oracle，评估了40个指标并发现ROF是最佳单一预测器，最后将ROF与三个结构正则化器组合为CROF分数用于离线检查点选择。

Result: CROF选出的世界模型训练的基于模型的A2C策略比无模型A2C基线高出约24.5回报点，同时减少了约65倍的真实环境交互次数；该世界模型还驱动了强大的零样本CEM-MPC策略。

Conclusion: ROF和CROF能够有效预测世界模型的闭环性能，基于模型的方法可以在大幅减少真实环境交互的情况下取得优于无模型方法的性能。

Abstract: We study how to predict the downstream closed-loop performance of a learned latent world model from validation-time diagnostics alone. Choosing the right checkpoint from a world-model training run is difficult: validation loss and multi-step prediction RMSE keep improving long after closed-loop performance has collapsed. We present a suite of structural validation-time diagnostics drawn from optimal-control theory and apply them to Gymnasium's LunarLander v3, which features shaped rewards. We train an RSSM [5, 4] world model on it and treat per checkpoint CEM-MPC return as the oracle for closed-loop quality. By evaluating 40 metrics against this oracle, we find that the strongest single predictor is the Reward Observability Fraction (ROF), which measures the reward predictor's dependence on the observable subspace. We combine ROF with three structural regularizers into a single-number offline checkpoint selection score, the Composite Reward Observability Fraction (CROF). The CROF-selected world model trains a model-based A2C policy that beats a fairly evaluated model-free A2C baseline by ~24.5 return points while using ~65x fewer real-environment interactions, and the same world model also drives a strong zero-shot CEM-MPC policy. Code and data: https://github.com/nsmoly/LunarLander_RSSM.

</details>


### [134] [IonSense-QKG: A Quantum-Readiness Metadata Framework for Lithium-Ion Battery Dataset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01286)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了IonSense-QKG，一个用于锂离子电池数据集发现的量子就绪元数据框架。该框架从EV-Battery-IonSense索引出发，为公共电池数据集丰富量子相关元数据（包括任务类型、感知模态、化学体系、标签可用性、序列类型、预处理要求、候选量子编码、估计量子比特范围及NISQ可行性），并引入透明的量子就绪评分（Quantum Readiness Score）将数据集排序，作为混合量子-经典电池基准测试的候选资源。框架支持基于查询的数据集发现，并发布了元数据表、评分脚本、鲁棒性检查、链接检查工具和SQL风格查询示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共锂离子电池数据集在化学体系、模态、规模、标签质量、序列结构和预处理复杂度等方面存在显著差异，这些差异直接影响数据集是否适用于近期的混合量子-经典机器学习工作流。现有缺乏一个系统性的、面向量子计算就绪性的数据集发现和评估框架，因此需要将数据集选择定位为数据管理问题，为数据驱动的量子电池分析提供可复现的基础。

Method: 基于EV-Battery-IonSense索引，为公共电池数据集记录丰富量子相关元数据，包括任务类型、感知模态、化学体系、标签可用性、序列类型、预处理要求、候选量子编码、估计量子比特范围和NISQ可行性。引入透明的量子就绪评分（Quantum Readiness Score）作为数据集选择的启发式方法，而非量子优势的证据。框架支持通过SQL风格查询对丰富后的元数据进行基于查询的数据集发现。

Result: 框架实现了基于查询的元数据发现，能够识别适用于紧凑量子特征映射、量子时间序列工作流、有限标签异常检测和未来电池健康基准测试的数据集。发布的工件包括元数据表、评分脚本、鲁棒性检查、链接检查工具和SQL风格查询示例。

Conclusion: IonSense-QKG将数据集选择重新定位为数据管理问题，并为数据中心的量子电池分析提供了可复现的基础。该框架旨在作为数据集选择启发式工具，帮助研究者识别适用于混合量子-经典机器学习工作流的电池数据集。

Abstract: Public lithium-ion battery datasets are increasingly used for state-of-health estimation, remaining-useful-life prediction, anomaly detection, electrochemical diagnostics, second-life analytics, and battery safety research. However, these datasets vary substantially in chemistry, modality, scale, label quality, sequence structure, access status, and preprocessing complexity. These differences directly affect whether a dataset is feasible for near-term hybrid quantum-classical machine-learning workflows.
  This paper presents IonSense-QKG, a quantum-readiness metadata framework for lithium-ion battery dataset discovery. Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>


### [135] [A Memory Efficient Unified Algorithm for Online Learning of Linear Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02050)
*Yuval Ran-Milo,Angelos Assos,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对一般未知线性动力系统(LDS)的在线预测问题，提出了一种参数复杂度为$\widetilde{O}(k)$的统一在线算法，其中$k$是不稳定复杂性（即实稳定区间外的特征值及非半单模态的数量）。理论证明当$k$较小时预测有意义且稳定化可行，并给出了下界：任何基于滤波器的预测器至少需要$k$个滤波器。实验表明该方法在相同参数预算下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测中稳定化一般未知线性动力系统(LDS)的挑战出发，研究在线预测这一自然先决条件。目标是实现次线性遗憾，且内存占用适应动力学的内在复杂性（即低不稳定复杂性$k$），而非全隐藏状态维度。

Method: 提出一种统一的在线算法，可处理所有LDS（包括不可对角化、具有复数或爆炸模态的系统），可学习参数数量为$\widetilde{O}(k)$。同时证明了$k$是有效的复杂度度量，任何基于滤波器的预测器至少需要$k$个滤波器。

Result: 理论证明：当$k$较小时预测有意义且稳定化可行；高不稳定复杂性的系统无法在不使用指数级大控制的情况下稳定化。实验表明，在高维系统上，所提方法在相同参数预算下显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 在线预测对于稳定化而言，恰好在不稳定复杂性$k$较小时才有意义。$k$是有效的复杂度度量，所提算法实现了与$k$匹配的参数效率。

Abstract: Motivated by the challenge of stabilizing a general unknown linear dynamical system (LDS) from observations, we study the natural prerequisite of online prediction. Our goal is to achieve sublinear regret with a memory footprint that adapts to the intrinsic complexity of the dynamics rather than the full hidden -- state dimension. We focus on the practically central regime of systems with low instability complexity -- eigenvalues outside the real stable interval that do not decay rapidly, together with non-semisimple modes-potentially embedded in an otherwise stable real spectrum of much higher dimension; we write $k$ for this count. This regime is the primary setting in which stabilization is plausible: we show that many systems with high instability complexity cannot be stabilized without exponentially large controls. Thus, prediction is meaningful for stabilization precisely when the instability complexity is small. Within this regime, we introduce a unified online algorithm that handles every LDS (including non-diagonalizable systems with complex or exploding modes) with a learnable parameter count of $\widetilde{O}(k)$. Finally, we prove a lower bound showing that $k$ is a valid complexity measure: any filter-based predictor needs at least $k$ filters. Experiments corroborate our theory: on a high-dimensional system, our predictor sharply outperforms prior methods at an equal parameter budget.

</details>


### [136] [A Novel Machine Learning Approach for Central Nervous System Tumor Classification from DNA Methylation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01307)
*Paulo R. Ferreira,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,Laís dos Santos Gonçalves,William Borges Domingues,Lucas Petitemberte de Souza,Mariana B. Michalowski,Vinicius F. Campos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合稀疏随机投影降维与多项逻辑回归的甲基化分类方法，在CNS肿瘤分类任务上，91类分类准确率达86%（参考方法82%），家族级准确率达93%（参考方法88%），显著优于现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA甲基化分析已成为中枢神经系统（CNS）肿瘤分类的有力手段，但仍面临跨队列可迁移性、方法论正确性和鲁棒多类评估等关键挑战。

Method: 提出一种机器学习方法，结合**稀疏随机投影（Sparse Random Projection）**进行降维，并使用**多项逻辑回归（Multinomial Logistic Regression）**进行分类。

Result: 在2801样本参考队列上，分层3折交叉验证平均准确率96%；在1104样本独立临床评估队列上，91类级准确率86%（参考方法82%），甲基化家族级准确率93%（参考方法88%），分别提升约4和5个百分点。

Conclusion: 所提模型在更强的机器学习方法论基础上，在各个评估设置下均持续优于先前最优方法，能够实质性提升CNS肿瘤分类的可靠性，对临床癌症亚型分配、治疗方案选择和临床决策具有重要影响。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation. In this work, we propose a novel and methodologically rigorous machine-learning approach for methylation-based CNS tumor classification that combines Sparse Random Projection for dimensionality reduction with multinomial logistic regression for classification. We evaluate the proposed approach in the same general experimental setting established by a widely used reference classifier. On the 2,801-sample reference cohort, our method achieves a mean accuracy of 96\% under stratified 3-fold cross-validation. On the independent 1,104-sample clinical evaluation cohort, it reaches 86\% accuracy at the 91-class level and 93\% when predictions are evaluated at the methylation class family level. These results improve upon the corresponding state-of-the-art reference figures of 82\% class-level concordance and 88\% family-level concordance, yielding absolute gains of approximately 4 and 5 percentage points, respectively. This improvement is clinically relevant: in a diagnostic setting, a 5-point increase in correct tumor classification can directly affect cancer subtype assignment and, in turn, influence treatment selection and downstream clinical decision-making. Our results show that the proposed model, grounded in stronger methodological practice in machine learning, consistently outperforms the previous state of the art across evaluation settings and can materially improve the reliability of CNS tumor classification.

</details>


### [137] [ART for Diffusion Sampling: Continuous-Time Control and Actor-Critic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02137)
*Yilie Huang,Wenpin Tang,Xun Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ART-RL方法，通过将扩散采样中的时间步分配建模为连续时间控制问题，并引入强化学习框架学习最优时间扭曲率，从而在保持采样管道不变的情况下，仅通过改变时间步网格即可一致提升样本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散采样中的时间步分配通常采用均匀或手工设计的固定方案，这些方案无法根据采样轨迹动态调整，导致次优的采样质量。

Method: 提出自适应重参数化时间(ART)的连续时间控制公式，将采样时钟速度作为控制量，使均匀网格在学到的时钟上诱导出自适应时间步。进一步引入ART-RL，将调度学习转化为连续时间强化学习问题，采用高斯策略并推导出可实现的actor-critic更新规则。

Result: 在从低维控制到图像生成的实验中，ART-RL仅通过改变时间步网格即可一致提升样本质量，且学到的调度展现出跨采样预算、数据集、求解器、管道和表示空间的广泛泛化能力。

Conclusion: ART-RL提供了一种原则性的时间步分配方法，能够在不需要修改采样管道其余部分的情况下显著提升扩散采样性能，且学到的调度具有良好的迁移性。

Abstract: We study timestep allocation for score-based diffusion sampling, where a learned reverse-time dynamics is discretized on a finite grid. Uniform and hand-crafted schedules are standard choices, but they rely on fixed prescriptions and can therefore be suboptimal. To address this limitation, we propose Adaptive Reparameterized Time (ART), a continuous-time control formulation that learns a time change by treating the speed of the sampling clock as the control, so that a uniform grid on the learned clock induces adaptive timesteps in the original diffusion time. Based on a leading-order Euler error surrogate, ART provides a principled objective for allocating timesteps along the sampling trajectory. To solve this deterministic control problem, we introduce ART-RL, an auxiliary randomized formulation with Gaussian policies that turns schedule learning into a continuous-time reinforcement learning problem. We prove that the randomized ART-RL formulation is equivalent to ART at the optimizer level, in the sense that its optimal Gaussian policy recovers the optimal ART time-warping rate through its mean. We further establish policy evaluation and policy improvement characterizations and derive trajectory-based moment identities that yield implementable actor--critic updates for learning the schedule. Across experiments ranging from controlled low-dimensional settings to image generation, ART-RL can be plugged into existing diffusion samplers by changing only the timestep grid, consistently improving sample quality over strong baseline schedules at matched budgets while leaving the rest of the sampling pipeline unchanged. The learned schedules also exhibit broad generalization, transferring without retraining across sampling budgets, datasets, solvers, pipelines, and representation spaces.

</details>


### [138] [Predicting Early Stages Of Alzheimer's Disease And Identifying Key Biomarkers Using Deep Artificial Neural Network And Ensemble Of Machine Learning Methodologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02142)
*Debopriya Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于机器学习的阿尔茨海默病早期检测模型，使用ADNI数据，通过迭代插补处理缺失值、Borderline SVM-SMOTE解决类不平衡、包装式和嵌入式方法进行特征选择，构建了以逻辑回归、Extra Trees、Bagging KNN和LightGBM为基分类器的堆叠集成模型及人工神经网络，旨在找出最佳分类器及重要生物标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病早期症状轻微易与正常衰老混淆，导致多数患者确诊时病情已进展，而目前尚无完全治愈方法，因此早期检测对于更好地管理病情和及时采取干预措施至关重要。

Method: 使用ADNI数据集，采用迭代插补填补缺失值，Borderline SVM-SMOTE处理类不平衡，包装法和嵌入式法进行特征选择，构建由逻辑回归、Extra Trees、Bagging KNN和LightGBM组成的堆叠集成模型，同时训练人工神经网络进行对比。

Result: 使用精确率、召回率、F1分数和AUC-ROC等指标对各模型性能进行了比较评估。

Conclusion: 本研究旨在确定阿尔茨海默病早期诊断的最佳分类器，并识别有助于早期诊断的重要生物标志物。

Abstract: Alzheimers disease (AD) is a brain disorder that develops slowly and mainly affects memory, thinking, language, and daily activities. It is one of the most common causes of dementia and creates many difficulties for patients as well as their families. In the early stage, the symptoms are often mild and may look like normal ageing. For this reason, many people are diagnosed late, when the disease has already progressed. At present, there is no complete cure for AD. Still, early detection can help doctors manage the condition better and take suitable steps at the right time. In this study, a machine learning model is proposed to detect the early stages of Alzheimers disease using clinical details, neuropsychological test scores, and neuroimaging-related measures. The data used in this work is collected from the Alzheimers Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI). As the dataset has missing values, iterative imputation is applied to fill them. The dataset also has class imbalance, which is handled using Borderline SVM-SMOTE. After that, feature selection is carried out using wrapper-based and embedded methods so that only important features are used for training. The selected features are divided into training and testing sets, and feature scaling is applied. A stacking ensemble model is developed using Logistic Regression, Extra Trees, Bagging KNN, and LightGBM as base classifiers. Along with this, an artificial neural network is also trained on the same dataset. The performance of these models is compared using precision, recall, F1-score, and AUC-ROC. This study aims to find the best classifier and also identify important biomarkers that may help in the early diagnosis of Alzheimers disease.

</details>


### [139] [From Approximation to Emergence: A Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01311)
*Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提供了一个统一的、以证明为导向的现代深度学习理论综述，从近似、优化和泛化的经典基础出发，系统梳理了过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐和涌现等当代机制，构建了一个连贯的研究叙事框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已超越了任何单一数学解释的能力范围，现有文献散落且缺乏统一的理论框架，亟需一个整合性的、以证明为导向的理论综述来系统梳理该领域的发展脉络。

Method: 采用统一的叙事框架组织广泛文献，通过每个理论所控制的对象、使其成立的前提条件以及未能解释的现象三个维度来审视每个理论。

Result: 构建了一幅关于当前深度学习理论的严谨地图，展示了其强大、不完整且日益聚焦于学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练中涌现的特点。

Conclusion: 深度学习理论正处于一个从经典理论向以涌现机制为核心的新范式的转型期，规模、数据、架构和训练之间的相互作用是理解现代深度学习的关键。

Abstract: Deep learning has outgrown any single mathematical explanation. From Approximation to Emergence develops a unified, proof-oriented account of modern deep learning theory, tracing a path from the classical foundations of approximation, optimization, and generalization to the contemporary mechanisms of overparameterization, robustness, generative modeling, transformers, in-context learning, scaling laws, interpretability, alignment, and emergence. Rather than presenting isolated results, the book organizes a broad literature into a coherent research narrative: each theory is examined through the object it controls, the assumptions that make it valid, and the phenomena it leaves unexplained. Written for researchers, graduate students, and mathematically trained practitioners, this monograph offers a rigorous map of deep learning theory as it stands today: powerful, incomplete, and increasingly centered on the question of how learned mechanisms arise from scale, data, architecture, and training.

</details>


### [140] [Black-Box Inference of LLM Architectural Properties with Restrictive API Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01313)
*Christopher Ellis,Shreyas Chaudhari,Mei-Yu Wang,Leighton Barnes,Giulia Fanti,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NightVision攻击方法，在仅能获取单个token对数概率和TTFT测量的限制性API下，通过共同集提示和频谱分析估算LLM的隐藏维度（平均相对误差23%），并结合TTFT估计深度和参数量（对30亿以上参数模型误差53%），证明当前API限制不足以隐藏模型架构细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业LLM提供商限制了API（仅暴露单个logit、禁用logit bias），但先前工作表明在更开放的API下可恢复架构细节。本文旨在探究在更严格的API限制下是否仍能恢复LLM的架构参数。

Method: NightVision方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 共同集提示（common set prompting）技术，通过多个提示让模型对同一组输出token产生log概率，然后对这些结果进行频谱分析来推断隐藏维度；(2) 利用端到端TTFT（time to first token）测量和估计出的隐藏维度来估算模型深度和参数量。

Result: 在32个开源LLM上评估，隐藏维度平均相对误差23%（MoE模型为9%）；对超过30亿参数的模型，深度和参数量估计误差在53%以内。通过消融实验展示了精度随token预算和模型属性的变化规律。

Conclusion: 当前LLM API的限制不足以完全混淆底层模型的架构细节，模型架构参数仍可在限制性API下被有效恢复。

Abstract: In practice, most commercial LLM providers do not publicly release details of underlying LLM architectures. However, prior work has shown that given limited API access to an LLM (namely, top-$k$ logits and/or a logit bias function), one can recover certain architectural details of an LLM, such as the hidden dimension of the feed-forward network. Perhaps in response to these results, most commercial LLM providers have restricted their APIs to expose only the single logit for each decoded token, and they no longer give users the ability to bias logits. We show that even under current restrictive APIs, several architectural parameters are still recoverable. We present NightVision, an attack that uses restrictive black-box API access to estimate the hidden dimension, depth, and parameter count of an LLM. Algorithmically, NightVision relies on a novel common set prompting technique in which multiple prompts expose log probabilities for the same set of output tokens; a spectral analysis of these results is used to infer hidden dimension. NightVision additionally uses end-to-end time to first token (TTFT) measurements and the estimated hidden dimension to estimate depth and parameter count. We empirically evaluate NightVision on 32 open-source LLMs, recovering hidden dimension to within 23% average relative error across all models (9% on MoE models), and depth and parameter count to within 53% for models exceeding three billion parameters. We run extensive ablations to demonstrate how these accuracies scale with token budget and model properties. Overall, our results suggest that current LLM APIs are not sufficiently restricted to fully obfuscate the architectural details of their underlying models.

</details>


### [141] [Multi-modal Rail Crossing Safety Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01365)
*Paimon Goulart,Chansong Lim,Nícolas Roque dos Santos,Yue Dong,Sheldon Peterson,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态AI系统，通过融合铁路道口的视觉图像和官方事故报告等结构化数据，实现对道口安全性的鲁棒评估。系统采用路由微调紧凑型视觉语言模型（VLM）管道，在HIGH-RISK/LOW-RISK分类上达到macro F1=0.757，FRA安全评分估计的RMSE=0.078、相关系数=0.492，且定性结果与领域专家评估一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否仅凭铁路道口图像进行安全评估，以及引入结构化数据（如官方事故报告）能否提升评估能力，最终构建一个与专家意见和FRA安全评分标准一致的多模态AI安全评估系统。

Method: 提出一套概念验证管道，涵盖数据准备、多模态融合、不同学习范式等关键研究挑战；采用路由微调紧凑型VLM管道，融合视觉图像与结构化事故数据，进行高风险/低风险分类和FRA安全评分回归估计。

Result: 高风险/低风险分类macro F1=0.757；FRA安全评分估计RMSE=0.078，相关系数=0.492；定性结果与领域专家评估一致。

Conclusion: 提出的多模态融合VLM管道能有效对齐专家意见和FRA标准进行铁路道口安全评估，验证了引入结构化数据对提升安全评估能力的价值。

Abstract: Given one or more images of a railway crossing, can we leverage visual cues that allow us to robustly estimate how safe it is? Can we improve our ability to do so by introducing structured data (such as official accident reports) about the accident history of that crossing into our models? In this work, we explore how to best answer those questions towards building an AI system that can ingest multi-modal data for railway crossings and provide safety assessment and scores that align with expert opinion and with safety scoring used by the Federal Railroad Administration (FRA). To that end, we propose a proof-of-concept pipeline that delivers on that goal, while at the same time exploring and tackling a number of critical research challenges that pertain to different parts of the pipeline, from data preparation to different learning paradigms that can allow us to realize such a system. Indicatively, our proposed system identifies HIGH-RISK and LOW-RISK crossings with a macro F1 score of 0.757 and estimates FRA-based safety scores with an RMSE of 0.078 and correlation of 0.492 using a routed fine-tuned compact VLM pipeline, while producing qualitative results that align with domain-expert assessment.

</details>


### [142] [How Should Transformers Encode Numeric Values in Electronic Health Records?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01391)
*Maria Elkjær Montgomery,Christian Igel,Mikkel Odgaard,Martin Sillesen,Mads Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Transformer模型中数值编码的离散、连续和混合策略，发现混合令牌方法在精度与鲁棒性间取得最佳平衡，且最优分箱数服从与数据集大小相关的幂律分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer模型在处理电子健康记录数据中的数值编码时缺乏系统性比较，需要明确不同编码策略在精度、稳定性和灵活性之间的权衡。

Method: 在真实EHR数据中嵌入合成算术任务，并结合真实临床预测任务，系统比较离散、连续和混合数值编码策略，分析数值-概念交互建模、分箱投影等方法的表现。

Result: 显式建模数值-概念交互在精度敏感任务上表现最佳但受架构限制；混合令牌方法（分箱后投影）是更稳健的选择，最优分箱数服从与数据集大小相关的幂律；模型表现出"足够好"的数值计算而非精确算术。

Conclusion: 在实践部署中鲁棒性和可用性通常比最大数值精度更重要，建议以混合令牌方法作为默认编码策略。

Abstract: How do we encode numeric values in transformer-based sequence processing, particularly in electronic health record (EHR) data?
